# FOREWORD

The Transmission Code defines the operational procedures and principles governing the relationship between the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) and all Transmission System Users. These Users may include Producers, Storage Facility Operators, Storage Representatives, RES and High-Efficiency CHP Aggregation Entities, Suppliers, the Distribution System Operator, the Transmission System Owner, the Distribution System Owner, Consumers connected to the Transmission System, and Demand Response Representatives. The Transmission Code is a document containing precisely defined procedures for the Operation of the Cyprus Power System, enabling its control and regulation over short-term, medium-term, and long-term horizons under conditions of safety, reliability, and minimal possible cost. Daily procedures are established for both planning and operational purposes, covering normal and emergency conditions. The Code provides System Operators with the necessary means to consistently achieve the instantaneous balancing of Generation and Demand within the liberalized Electricity Market environment of Cyprus.

The Transmission Code is designed to enable the development, maintenance, and operation of an efficient, coordinated, and economical Transmission System, as well as to facilitate the operation of the Electricity Market. It is understood that the Transmission Code shall be regarded as the definitive specification of standard and mandatory practice (specifically from a technical standpoint) for all Users and for the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) itself, regarding the planning, operation, and use of the Transmission System.

The General Provisions (T0) ensure, as far as practicable, that the various sections of the Transmission Code are mutually complementary in their entirety and practically enforceable. The General Provisions contain stipulations regarding the review and modification of the Transmission Code, as well as other provisions of a general nature.

The Transmission Code comprises:

* **(a)** The **Connection Conditions to the Transmission System (T1)**, which specify the minimum technical, design, and operational criteria to be satisfied by the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) at connection points, as well as by Users currently connected to or seeking connection to the Transmission System.
* **(b)** The **Planning Rules (T2)**, which fundamentally mandate the submission of specific information by Users to the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) to enable the TSOC to conduct strategic planning studies and plan the expansion of the Transmission System.
* **(c)** A suite of **Operational Rules**, dealing with Demand Forecasting and RES Generation Forecasting; the outage coordination of the Transmission System and User Systems for construction, repair, and maintenance; methods for Demand reduction/management; the reporting of planned switching operations and unforeseen contingencies such as faults; procedures for submitting written post-fault incident reports; and procedures for audits, investigations, and monitoring conducted by the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC).
* **(d)** Another suite of rules concerning **Safety Coordination**, detailing procedural arrangements for coordinating personnel engaged in work on the Transmission System. A third group of rules relates to technical specifications...

<!-- PAGE 41 -->

...and metering procedures related to settlement requirements, in accordance with the Electricity Market Rules.

(e) Rules for notifying the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) regarding any change in the Operating Characteristics of Dispatchable Generating Units and other Market Participants, to enable the TSOC to fulfill its obligations within the framework of the Balancing Market. These provisions of T8.3 operate complementarily to the provisions of the Electricity Market Rules.

(f) Provisions regarding the content and format of Instructions issued by the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) within the framework of the Balancing Market, as well as the procedures to be followed by the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) and Users for communication regarding Instructions.

This Foreword is provided to Users and prospective Users for informational purposes only and does not constitute part of the Transmission Rules.

# T0 – GENERAL PROVISIONS

## T0.1 INTRODUCTION

**T0.1.1** The General Provisions (T0) include provisions that have general application across all conditions of the Transmission Code. Their purpose is to ensure, as far as possible, the unified operation and practical cooperation among the various provisions of the Transmission Code for the benefit of all **USERS**. Terms written in **CAPITAL BOLD LETTERS** shall be interpreted in accordance with their definition in the **GLOSSARY OF TERMS AND DEFINITIONS** of the Transmission Code.

---

## T0.2 OBJECTIVES

**T0.2.1** The objectives of the General Provisions are as follows:

(a) To ensure, as far as possible, the unified operation and practical cooperation among the various conditions of the Transmission Code for the benefit of the operation of the Power System, the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (**TSOC**), the Distribution System Operator, and all **USERS**.

(b) To provide a set of principles governing the existing state and the development of the Transmission Code and other related matters as approved by **CERA**.

---

## T0.3 SCOPE OF APPLICATION

The General Provisions apply to the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (**TSOC**), the Distribution System Operator, the Transmission and Distribution System Owners, **CERA**, and all other **USERS** referenced in the individual chapters of the Transmission Code.

---

## T0.4 PURPOSE AND LEGISLATION

**T0.4.1** The Transmission Code is designed to regulate all technical matters concerning the operation and use of the Transmission System, and the technical matters of Facilities and/or Plant connected to the Transmission System or to the Distribution System that relate to the operation and use of the Transmission System.

**T0.4.2** Pursuant to Article 85(2) of the Law, **CERA** may from time to time issue directions to the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (**TSOC**) regarding:

(a) matters that may be specified in the Transmission Code; and  
(b) the review and re-examination of the Transmission Code by the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (**TSOC**).

**T0.4.3** **TSOC** shall not publish the Transmission Code until **CERA**:

(a) completes the consultation process regarding the Transmission Code;  
(b) approves the Transmission Code.

**T0.4.4** Pursuant to Article 86 of the Law, the Transmission Code:

(a) Governs the technical requirements and constraints to be applied by License holders who wish to connect to the System

---

*Edition 1.1.0 | T0 – General Provisions | Page 43*

<!-- PAGE 44 -->

Transmission or use of the Transmission System for the transmission of electricity

(b) ensure that the technical conditions applied to Licensees wishing to connect to or use the Transmission System do not create undue discrimination among Licensees;

(c) promote efficiency, reliability, and economy in the operation and development of the Transmission System;

(d) are fully harmonized with the provisions of Regulation (EU) 2019/943, where applicable.

---

### T0.5 REVIEW AND AMENDMENT OF THE TRANSMISSION RULES

**T0.5.1.1** In accordance with Article 87, paragraph (1) of the Law, following the approval of the Transmission Rules, the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) shall, from time to time, review and, when deemed necessary, recommend amendments to the Transmission Rules:

(a) when CERA requests such a review; or  
(b) when the TSOC deems that such a review is desirable, based on views expressed by Licensees or other interested parties; or  
(c) when the DSO deems that such a review is desirable; or  
(d) for their harmonization with the provisions of Regulation (EU) 2019/943, where applicable.

**T0.5.1.2** Any review of the Transmission Rules shall be conducted by the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) in consultation with an informal advisory committee consisting of representatives of Licensees and other interested parties directly affected by the scope of such review, the composition of which shall be determined by Decision of CERA.

**T0.5.1.3** The TSOC, after duly considering the written or oral views of the representatives of the said informal advisory committee, subsequently decides and places under public consultation the proposed amendments relating to the scope of such review.

**T0.5.1.4** In the event that, following the above procedure, the TSOC deems it necessary to introduce amendments to the Transmission Rules, it shall publish on its official website its proposal for the amendment of the Transmission Rules, which it submits for approval to CERA together with any other relevant information CERA may deem necessary.

**T0.5.1.5** CERA issues a Decision by which it may approve, reject, or modify the TSOC proposal submitted to CERA pursuant to the provisions of the preceding paragraph.

**T0.5.1.6** The TSOC publishes the amendments to the Transmission Rules in accordance with the relevant Decision of CERA and the amended consolidated text of the Transmission Rules.

**T0.5.1.7** None of the amendments to the Transmission Rules proposed by the TSOC shall enter into force until approved and published by CERA.

---
*Version 1.1.0 | T0 – General Provisions | Page 44*

<!-- PAGE 45 -->

| Version 1.1.0 | T0 – General Provisions | Page 45 |
|:---|:---:|---:|

### T0.6 PUBLIC CONSULTATION PROCEDURE

**T0.6.1** The TSOC publishes a notice regarding the commencement of a public consultation on proposed amendments to the Transmission Rules in two widely circulated newspapers in the Republic, as well as on the TSOC website.

**T0.6.2** Through this notice, the TSOC informs that the relevant documents and information have been posted on the TSOC website and invites any interested party to submit to the TSOC, within the consultation period, information, comments, or proposals related to the proposed amendment to the Transmission Rules.

**T0.6.3** The duration and the manner of conducting the public consultation are announced in the publication.

---

### T0.7 EXPLANATION OF TRANSMISSION RULES

**T0.7.1** In the event that a User desires an additional explanation regarding the scope and application of the provisions of the Transmission Rules, they may request such an explanation from the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC), and the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus must provide the User with the explanation of the relevant provision.

**T0.7.2** If the User considers that the explanation provided by the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus pursuant to T0.7.1 is incomplete, the User may request additional clarifications from the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus.

**T0.7.3** If the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus deems that such explanations are also useful to other Users, the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus shall publish this information on its website or through other appropriate means.

---

### T0.8 DEROGATIONS

**T0.8.1** If a User considers that they are unable or will be unable to comply with the provisions of the Transmission Rules, that User must immediately inform the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus of this inability and must, in accordance with the provisions of T0.8.2, make every necessary effort to resolve the non-compliance as quickly as possible.

**T0.8.2** A non-compliance exists in relation to:
* **(a)** Facilities and/or Plant connected to the Transmission System or the Distribution System, caused solely or mainly as a result of an amendment to the Transmission Rules.
* **(b)** Facilities and/or Plant that are connected, approved for connection, or for which connection approval exists to the Transmission System or the Distribution System, and the User believes either that it is unreasonable (from a technical and economic perspective) to require the resolution of the non-compliance, or that an extended period of time must be secured for resolving the non-compliance. The User must request a derogation from CERA from this condition in accordance with T0.8.3 and shall...

<!-- PAGE 46 -->

**Version 1.1.0** | **T0 – General Provisions** | **Page 46**

... notifies a copy of their application to the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC).

**T0.8.3** An application for a derogation from any provision of the Transmission Rules shall include the following:

(a) Revision number and date of the provision of the Transmission Rules in which non-compliance has been observed or is anticipated to occur.  
(b) Identification of the Facilities and/or Equipment for which a derogation is being sought and, where relevant, the nature and extent of the non-compliance.  
(c) Identification of the provision which the User is or will be unable to comply with.  
(d) Reason for the non-compliance.  
(e) Date by which compliance will be achieved (if rectification of non-compliance is possible) pursuant to T0.8.2 (b).

**T0.8.4** If the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) considers that compliance with any provision of the Transmission Rules is or will be impossible, then, in accordance with the remaining provisions of T0.8, it shall take all necessary measures to rectify the non-compliance as soon as possible.

**T0.8.5** In the event that a User requests a derogation, the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) shall forward all information specified in T0.8.3 to CERA.

**T0.8.6** CERA, upon receipt of a derogation application, shall immediately examine the request and, if it deems the grounds for the derogation to be justified, shall grant the derogation. Excluded are cases where there may be or are likely to be material adverse impacts on the security and stability of the Transmission System, or where an unreasonable increase in the operational costs of the Transmission System or Users is caused. When examining a User's derogation application, CERA may communicate with that User and/or the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) for clarifications regarding the request or to discuss potential modifications to the application.

A derogation from any provision of the Transmission Rules shall include the following:

(a) Revision number and date of the provision of the Transmission Rules for which derogation is requested.  
(b) Identification of the provision which the User is or will be unable to comply with.  
(c) Identification of the Facilities and/or Equipment for which a derogation is being examined and, where relevant, the nature and extent of the non-compliance, including compliance with alternative provisions.  
(d) Reason for the non-compliance.  
(e) Date prior to which the derogation shall be deemed completed, if rectification of compliance is possible, or the expiry date of the applicable derogation.

**T0.8.7** In the event that a derogation is granted pursuant to T0.8, the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) and/or the User (as applicable) shall be relieved of the obligation to comply with the corresponding provision of the Transmission Rules and shall not be held liable for such non-compliance.

<!-- PAGE 47 -->

...but must comply with any alternative condition included in the derogation.

**T0.8.8** The Transmission System Operator of Cyprus maintains a register of all derogations approved by CERA, specifying:
(a) Name of the natural or legal person to whom the derogation has been issued.
(b) The relevant condition of the Transmission Rules.
(c) Duration of the derogation.

The Transmission System Operator of Cyprus, acting in its reasonable judgment and in consultation with CERA, shall provide a copy of a User derogation to any User who submits a formal request to TSOC.

**T0.8.9** If significant changes in circumstances occur, CERA, on its own initiative or upon request by the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus or Users, may decide to review all existing derogations or derogations currently under consideration.

### T0.9 NON-COMPLIANCE OF USER PLANT AND/OR APPARATUS

**T0.9.1.1** In the event of a partial failure of a Plant and/or Apparatus causing non-compliance that does not necessarily affect the safe and reliable operation of the Facilities and/or Apparatus, the User shall immediately notify the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus of the non-compliance and submit the User's proposed schedule for resolving the non-compliance.

**T0.9.1.2** Where an emergency situation does not exist and the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus considers that a User's non-compliance, as referred to in T0.9.1.1, has a material effect on another User or Users, the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus shall advise the affected User or Users regarding the impact of the potential non-compliance on that User or those Users.

**T0.9.1.3** If the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus, acting in its reasonable judgment and taking into account the operating regime of the Transmission System as well as the views of the affected Users pursuant to T0.9.1.2, considers that the User's schedule for resolving the non-compliance is satisfactory, and the relevant defect does not cause or is not likely to cause a material impact on operational security and operational stability of the Transmission System or on other Users, and does not cause or is not likely to cause an unreasonable and unpredictable increase in the operating cost of the Transmission System or of Users, the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus may, for as long as it deems necessary, consider the User compliant with that specific condition of the Transmission Rules, and the User shall be deemed compliant.

**T0.9.1.4** If the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus, acting in its reasonable judgment, taking into account the operating regime of the Transmission System and the views of the affected Users pursuant to T0.9.1.2, considers that the User's schedule for resolving the non-compliance is unsatisfactory, the User shall submit an application for a derogation in accordance with T0.8.

**T0.9.2** In the event of a failure of a Plant and/or Apparatus that causes non-compliance and affects the safe and reliable operation of the Facilities and/or Apparatus, the User shall immediately inform the...

<!-- PAGE 48 -->

**Edition 1.1.0 T0 – General Provisions**  
**Page 48**

... Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) regarding the non-compliance and includes this non-compliance in the Declarations of Operating Capability Changes until the point in time when the non-compliance has been resolved.

### T0.9.3 
In case of disagreement between the User and the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC), the User shall submit a derogation application in accordance with T0.8.2.

---

## T0.10 ASSISTANCE FOR IMPLEMENTATION

### T0.10.1 
The Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) and Users shall adhere to and comply with the Transmission Rules approved by CERA.

### T0.10.2 
For the purpose of enforcing the Transmission Rules, the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) may, under certain conditions, require access rights outside the geographical boundaries of the Transmission System or may require the provision of services or facilities from Users specified in the Transmission Rules. Such services include, for example, the De-energisation and/or disconnection of Plant and/or Apparatus. It is desirable that such situations occur by way of exception, and, therefore, it is considered difficult to predefine precisely what the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) will request in order to be able to apply the Transmission Rules under these conditions.

### T0.10.3 
All Users referred to in the Transmission Rules must respect the letter and the spirit of the Transmission Rules, which includes the right of the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) to reasonable access rights, provision of services, and facilities specified in relevant agreements, as well as compliance with instructions that the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) may issue in attempting to implement the Transmission Rules.

### T0.10.4 
The Licence obliges the Distribution System Operator (DSO) to apply the Transmission Rules. To achieve this objective, the Distribution System Operator (DSO) may require rights of access outside its geographical boundaries, provision of services and facilities from Users, or issue instructions to Users, for example, to isolate or connect Plant or Apparatus. It is desirable that such situations occur by way of exception, and, therefore, it is considered difficult to predefine precisely what the Distribution System Operator (DSO) will request in order to be able to apply the Transmission Rules.

### T0.10.5 
All Users must act in accordance with the Transmission Rules and grant the Distribution System Operator (DSO) rights of access, provision of services, and facilities, and comply with such instructions, so as to enable the implementation of the Transmission Rules.

---

## T0.11 UNFORESEEN CIRCUMSTANCES

### T0.11.1 
When circumstances arise which are not provided for in the Transmission Rules, the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) and the

<!-- PAGE 49 -->

**Issue 1.1.0 | T0 – General Provisions | Page 49**

...Distribution System Operator, as the case may be and in accordance with prevailing conditions, shall consult directly and in good faith with all affected Users in an effort to find a mutually agreed course of action.

**T0.11.2** When available time is insufficient to reach an agreement on action between the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) or the Distribution System Operator (DSO) and Users, the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) or the Distribution System Operator (DSO) shall decide on the actions to be taken. Whenever the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) or the Distribution System Operator (DSO) makes such decisions, it shall consider everything relevant and pursuant to this paragraph regarding the views expressed by Users. In any case, the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) or the Distribution System Operator (DSO) shall act reasonably and in accordance with Good Electricity Industry Practice. Additionally, following such a decision, the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) or the Distribution System Operator (DSO), if requested, shall explain to any affected User the reasons compelling its actions.

**T0.11.3** Each User shall comply with the instructions of the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) and the Distribution System Operator (DSO) in such a situation, provided that the instructions are compatible with the technical characteristics of the User's System as registered in the Transmission Rules.

**T0.11.4** Whenever the Distribution System Operator (DSO) decides on actions regarding unforeseen situations, it shall take into account, as far as possible, the views expressed by Users, and in any case shall act reasonably in accordance with Good Electricity Industry Practice.

**T0.11.5** Each User shall comply with the instructions of the Distribution System Operator (DSO) in such a situation, provided that the instructions are compatible with the technical characteristics of the User's System as registered in the Transmission Rules.

### T0.12 HIERARCHY

**T0.12.1** In the event of any conflict between terms or provisions among the Regulation of the Electricity Market Law, the Electricity Law, the Electricity Development Law, the Regulations deriving therefrom, and the Transmission Rules, the terms or provisions of the Laws shall prevail, unless explicitly stated otherwise in the relevant Laws and Regulations.

**T0.12.2** In the event of any conflict between terms of the Transmission Rules and any contract, agreement, or arrangement between the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) or the Distribution System Operator (DSO), as the case may be, and a User, the terms of the Transmission Rules shall prevail, unless explicitly stated otherwise in the Transmission Rules or in the relevant contracts.

**T0.12.3** In the event of any conflict between terms of a CERA directive on the one hand and terms of the Transmission Rules on the other hand, the terms of the directive shall prevail under the following conditions:

<!-- PAGE 50 -->

(a) this instruction makes explicit reference to the specific condition of the Transmission Rules, and
(b) this instruction applies to the person to whom it has been issued.

---

### T0.13 OWNERSHIP OF FACILITIES AND/OR PLANT

References included in the Transmission Rules regarding the Facilities and/or Plant of a User also include Facilities and/or Plant used by the User pursuant to an agreement with third parties.

---

### T0.14 SYSTEM CONTROL

When a User System (or part thereof) is, pursuant to an agreement, under the control of the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC), then for matters of communication and operational timing adjustment, the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) may (solely for these reasons) treat the User System (or part thereof) as part of the Transmission System, but for the relationships between the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) and other Users, it continues to be considered a User System.

The above provisions also apply to the Distribution System Operator (DSO).

---

### T0.15 ILLEGALITY AND PARTIAL INVALIDITY

**T0.15.1** If any provisions of the Transmission Rules are held to be illegal or partially invalid for any reason, the validity and enforceability of the remaining provisions of the Transmission Rules shall not be affected.

**T0.15.2** If part of a provision of the Transmission Rules is held to be illegal or invalid, but the validity of the remainder of the provision is preserved if part of the wording is severed, the provision shall apply with such modification as necessary to make it valid and effective, without affecting the meaning and validity of any other provision of the Transmission Rules.

---

### T0.16 EMERGENCY SITUATIONS

Provisions of the Transmission Rules may be suspended, in whole or in part, during an Emergency Situation declared in accordance with the provisions of the Law.

\<End of T0\>
