<!-- PAGE 52 -->

# T1 - CONNECTION CONDITIONS TO THE TRANSMISSION SYSTEM

## T1.1 INTRODUCTION

**T1.1.1** The Connection Conditions to the Transmission System (T1) specify the minimum technical, design, and operational specifications with which every User connected or seeking to connect to the Transmission System must comply, the terminology and nomenclature of Facilities and Equipment, technical standards, performance standards, and data requirements. Furthermore, the Connection Conditions to the Transmission System specify the minimum technical, design, and operational specifications with which the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) shall comply regarding the portion of the Transmission System at the Connection Site, so that Users and prospective Users can design their Facilities and Equipment and install appropriate control systems and protection devices for their Facilities.

**T1.1.2** In addition to the Connection Conditions to the Transmission System, there are Connection Agreements, which include every specific detail regarding the User's connection to the Transmission System. Connection Agreements to the Transmission System require the contracting parties to comply with the provisions of the Transmission Rules, except in cases where a derogation has been granted under the General Provisions.

**T1.1.3** Connection Agreements to the Transmission System are based on the implementation of the Connection Procedure to the Transmission System. It is noted in this regard that the financial terms of the Connection Agreements to the Transmission System comply with the Charging Policy for Connection to the Transmission System.

**T1.1.4** The conditions and requirements, as described in the CERA-approved documents "Connection Procedure to the Transmission System" and "Charging Policy for Connection to the Transmission System", constitute a necessary supplement to the regulations provided under the provisions of the Law and in no case replace or amend them.

## T1.2 PURPOSE

The purpose of T1 is to specify the minimum technical, design, and operational specifications, as well as the fundamental rules for connection to the Transmission System for all Users of each equivalent category.

## T1.3 SCOPE

**T1.3.1** T1 applies to the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) and to Users, who within T1 include:

* **(a)** Generators, for Generating Units connected to the Transmission System as well as for Generating Units connected to the Distribution System for which a Connection Agreement has been concluded with TSOC;
* **(b)** Consumers connected to the Transmission System;
* **(c)** Transmission System Owner;
* **(d)** Distribution System Operator;
* **(e)** Storage Facility Operators connected to the Transmission System.

***

| **Version 1.1.0** | **T1 – Connection Conditions to the Transmission System** | **Page 52** |
|:---|:---:|---:|

<!-- PAGE 53 -->

**Version 1.1.0** | **T1 – Terms of Connection to the Transmission System** | **Page 53**

---

The above categories of Users are bound by T1 prior to generation, supply, absorption, or consumption, as applicable, and references to the various categories must consequently be understood as references to Users in accordance with the role they may acquire, as is the case with references to already connected Users.

**T1.3.2** Where the obligations of the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) to Users are stated in the Connection Terms to the Transmission System, and these obligations involve construction works and/or modifications of existing circuits, provision of signals or indications, protection schemes, and any other physical actions or works on the Transmission System, they shall be construed to mean that the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus undertakes the responsibility to cooperate with the Transmission System Owner for the works to be executed, applying the duties of the Transmission System Owner in accordance with the provisions of the Transmission System Protocol.

### T1.4 TRANSMISSION SUBSTATIONS

**T1.4.1** The User must provide land for the Intake Substation, as provided for in the Connection Agreements, which shall be located directly adjacent to the User's facilities or at another point acceptable to the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus for the construction of the Intake Substation and the installation of other necessary equipment for connecting the User's Facilities to the Transmission System.

**T1.4.2** Connection to the Transmission System must comply with the specifications set out in Chapter T2 and this Chapter T1. The connection method applied may meet specifications more stringent than the above when requested by the User and accepted by the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus.

**T1.4.3** The boundary between the Transmission System and the User's Facilities is generally defined in the drawings provided in Appendix B:

a) Separation between the Transmission System, Distribution System, and Independent Areas of Responsibility for Operational purposes (Appendix "B1").

b) Separation between the Transmission System, Distribution System, and Independent Areas of Responsibility for Ownership and Maintenance purposes (Appendix "B2").

In this regard, it is noted that the current boundaries of the Transmission System are defined in the TSOC Technical Directive "Areas of Responsibility of the Transmission System".

The separation between the Transmission and Distribution Systems for Operational purposes classifies the medium voltage feeders of Transmission Substations under the Distribution System. However, in this specific case, this does not relieve the DSO and the local Distribution System Control Centers from the obligation to inform and obtain the approval of the National Energy Control Center for all cases where Transmission Substations are momentarily paralleled, either directly by closing an automatic feeder circuit breaker or indirectly via another point in the Medium Voltage Network.

<!-- PAGE 54 -->

Provided that TSOC, by means of a circular, shall grant exemptions for paralleling of feeders where approval from the National Energy Control Center is not deemed necessary. The circular shall be revised from time to time depending on the prevailing system operating conditions.

### T1.5 IDENTIFICATION OF USER INSTALLATIONS

**T1.5.1** The designation of the Installation Site shall be determined by the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) in consultation with the User. In the event of a disagreement, the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus shall make the final and binding decision.

**T1.5.2** The terminology and nomenclature used regarding the Installations of the User connected to the Transmission System must comply with the standardized terminology of the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus, which has been specifically designed to ensure the avoidance of confusion regarding terminology and nomenclature. The User shall communicate its proposals regarding the terminology and nomenclature of its Installations and/or Apparatus to the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus. If the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus considers that the proposed terminology and nomenclature may cause confusion or does not comply with its standardized terminology, it shall notify the User of its replacement, which shall be applied to the User's Installations and/or Apparatus.

**T1.5.3** Every User is responsible for the provision, placement, and maintenance of clear and legible signage displaying the terminology and nomenclature of the Installations and Apparatus located at the User's Installation Site. The Transmission System Operator of Cyprus may visit and verify the correctness of the signage and at the same time reserves the right to periodically ensure the accuracy of the signage. For the avoidance of doubt, it is the obligation of Users to continuously ensure the correctness of their signage.

---

### T1.6 IMPORTANT TECHNICAL SPECIFICATIONS APPLICABLE TO USER INSTALLATIONS AND APPARATUS

**T1.6.1** The Installations and Apparatus of each User relating to its connection to the Transmission System must comply with:

* **(a)** the relevant European specifications;
* **(b)** if relevant European specifications do not exist, the specifications generally applied in European Union member states which are in force, in any case, at the entry into force of the User's Connection Agreement and are accepted by TSOC.

If, in the reasonable judgment of the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus, to ensure the safe and coordinated operation of the Installations and Apparatus of each User on the Transmission System, the application of supplementary requirements for the design of the User's Installations and Apparatus is required, the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus shall inform the User accordingly and the User shall comply with the supplementary requirements. Upon request by the User, the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus shall provide the necessary evidence demonstrating the need to apply supplementary standards and/or specifications.

---
*Issue 1.1.0 | T1 – Terms of Connection to the Transmission System | Page 54*

<!-- PAGE 55 -->

Every User shall submit a certification to the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus that its Facilities and Equipment concerning its connection to the Transmission System are in compliance with the Essential Requirements (Electromagnetic Compatibility) Regulations of 2003 (P.I. 600/2003), as derived from Article 59 of the "Essential Requirements to be Met by Specified Categories of Products" Law of 2002 (30(I)/2002) and its amendments.

**T1.6.2**  
In the event of a modification to the standards or specifications with which the User's Facilities and Equipment must comply pursuant to paragraph T1.6.1, the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus, taking into account the opinion of the involved Users, shall propose to CERA the actions that must be taken.

---

### T1.7 SPECIAL DESIGN AND PERFORMANCE SPECIFICATIONS

**T1.7.1**  
For the safe and stable operation of the Transmission System, the User's Facilities and Equipment are required to be designed in such a way as to be capable of meeting the operational requirements of the Transmission System across a range of different operating conditions.

#### T1.7.2 All Users

##### T1.7.2.1 Grounding

**T1.7.2.1.1**  
The grounding of all User Facilities and Equipment and the installation of the grounding system must be carried out at least in accordance with the guidelines contained in the "Guide for Safety in AC Substation Grounding", IEEE Std 80-2013, or the most recent relevant specifications applied in European Union countries that are accepted by TSOC.

**T1.7.2.1.2**  
The Transmission System Operator of Cyprus shall consult with each User regarding the exact specifications of the grounding system to be installed.

**T1.7.2.1.3**  
The earth electrodes of each User must be grounded by direct connection to the substation or to the central grounding system of the substation.

**T1.7.2.1.4**  
Each User shall submit to TSOC for approval a Certification from the person or organization that conducted the inspection of the User's Facilities and Equipment, confirming that the necessary precautions have been taken to ensure that any potential Voltage Rise will not be transferred outside the grounding zone, and that the grounding systems of Remote Transmission Facilities as well as the grounding systems of any potentially affected third party have been isolated from the User's installation area.

The User's Area of Responsibility shall not be permitted to be energized prior to the submission of the above Certification to the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus.

---

*Edition 1.1.0 | T1 – Terms of Connection to the Transmission System | Page 55*

<!-- PAGE 56 -->

**Edition 1.1.0** | **T1 – Terms of Connection to the Transmission System**
**Page 56**

---

**T1.7.2.1.5** The earthing system of each User must be connected to the earthing system of the Transmission Substation in such a way as to ensure effective bonding of the two earthing systems.

**T1.7.2.1.6** Each User guarantees that personnel working on the User's earthing system are appropriately trained to execute the relevant tasks in a safe manner.

### T1.7.2.2 Design

**T1.7.2.2.1** The design of the Facilities and Plant of each User must meet the following minimum specifications (for each applied Voltage level):

#### Table 1.1: Design Specifications for Facilities and Plant

| Parameter (Minimum) | 11kV | 22kV | 66kV | 132kV | 220kV |
| :--- | :---: | :---: | :---: | :---: | :---: |
| **Insulation Level, Lightning Impulse Voltage (kV) (1.2/50 μsec.)** | | | | | |
| i) Outdoor equipment | 95 | 125 | 325 | 650 | 1050 |
| ii) Indoor equipment | 75 | 125 | 325 | 650 | 1050 |
| **Insulation Level, Power Frequency Voltage (kV) (50Hz for 1 min)** | 28 | 50 | 140 | 270 | 485 |
| **Safety clearances of live metallic parts (mm Phase to Earth)** | 200 | 203 | 685 | 1270 | 2400 |
| **Height of live metallic parts above pedestrian walkways (mm)** | 5200 | 5200 | 6000 | 6700 | 7000 |
| **Height of bottom part of insulators without protective mesh above ground level (mm)** | 2440 | 2440 | 2440 | 2440 | 2440 |
| **Height of live conductors above roads (mm)** | 6700 | 6700 | 6700 | 7200 | 7200 |

Regarding the height of live conductors existing as of September 2004 above roads with 11kV installations, the minimum height is set at 5200mm. It is understood that compliance with the above levels will be gradually promoted whenever an opportunity for network development arises.

The minimum horizontal clearances between buildings or other structures and the centerline of overhead power transmission lines are specified as follows:

#### Table 1.2: Clearances for Overhead Lines

| Voltage | Minimum distance of buildings / structures from the centerline of the overhead line to each side |
| :--- | :--- |
| **Overhead lines 66kV** | $\ge 13\text{ meters}$ |
| **132kV** | $\ge 15.5\text{ meters}$ |
| **220kV** | $\ge 20\text{ meters}$ |

For transmission lines of another intermediate Voltage between 66kV and 220kV, the minimum distance to be maintained shall be calculated proportionally according to the above scale. Lines approved up to June 2005 are exempt.

<!-- PAGE 57 -->

**Version 1.1.0** | **T1 – Terms of Connection to the Transmission System** | **Page 57**

---

### T1.7.2.2.2 
The design of the Facilities and Plant of each User must withstand the short-circuit levels specified in paragraph T1.8.6.

### T1.7.2.3 Low Voltage Power Supply Cables and Wiring System

#### T1.7.2.3.1 
All multi-core control and protection cables installed on cable trays or underground must be provided with adequate metallic screening/armoring. At the base of the cabinets/cubicles, appropriate grounding devices for these surfaces/screens must be installed on both sides.

#### T1.7.2.3.2 
Low Voltage power supply cables and the auxiliary wiring system shall be routed from the Transmission Substation to the control building of each User via a mutually agreed underground route. Cables shall be laid in solid trenches reinforced with concrete capping or in another mutually agreed manner, up to the User's connection panel, which is located near the Transmission Substation.

### T1.7.2.4 Interlocking Arrangements

#### T1.7.2.4.1 
The locking mechanism in the open/closed position and/or interlocking devices shall be installed by the User on the appropriate switches, disconnectors, and/or circuit breakers (draw-out type) so as to achieve safety isolation of the facility from the power supply when required by TSOC. The details of the above requirements shall be specified at the design stage.

#### T1.7.2.4.2 
Existing Generating Stations and Transmission Substations currently in operation must comply with paragraph T1.7.2.4.1, subject to verification by TSOC.

### T1.7.2.5 Transformers Connected to the Transmission System

#### T1.7.2.5.1 
Producers' facilities shall be equipped with On-Load Tap Changer (OLTC) systems for all Generation Unit Transformers they possess. Similarly, Electricity Storage Facilities shall be equipped with On-Load Tap Changer (OLTC) systems for all transformers connected to the Transmission System. Consumers connected to the Transmission System should preferably be equipped with Automatic On-Load Tap Changer (OLTC) systems for all transformers connected to the Transmission System. The design specifications applied by Users for the operation of the on-load tap changing system on Transformers connected to the Transmission System must be approved by TSOC.

In the event that the User's design relates to the implementation of a system already operating within their facilities, separate approval is not required.

#### T1.7.2.5.2 
The windings of Transformers of Generation Units and transformers of Electricity Storage Facilities shall be star-connected (with the star point and neutral terminal brought out to a dedicated terminal) on the High Voltage side and in delta on the Low Voltage side.

<!-- PAGE 58 -->

**Issue 1.1.0** | **T1 – Terms of Connection to the Transmission System**  
**Page 58**

---

Other Transformers connected to the Transmission System are connected as follows:

*(a)* Transformers connected to the 66 kV Transmission System are connected in star (with the star point and the neutral point output brought to a separate terminal) on the Low Voltage side and in delta on the High Voltage side.  
*(b)* Transformers connected to the 132 kV or 220 kV Transmission System are connected in delta on the Low Voltage side and in star (with the star point and the neutral point output brought to a separate terminal) on the High Voltage side.  
*(c)* Inter-bus transformers are connected in star on both High and Low Voltage sides with a tertiary winding of suitable Loading Capacity connected in delta, or autotransformers with a permanently grounded common terminal and equipped with a tertiary winding of suitable Loading Capacity connected in delta.

**T1.7.2.5.3.1** The Low Voltage neutral points of all transformers connected to the 66 kV Transmission System must be effectively grounded.

**T1.7.2.5.3.2** The High Voltage neutral points of all transformers of the 132 kV or 220 kV Transmission System must be permanently grounded. The grounding of the Low Voltage side of these transformers must be provided by suitable grounding transformers.

**T1.7.2.5.4** The Transmission System Operator is responsible for arranging the connection of the trip mechanism of the High Voltage circuit breaker of the Transformer connected to the Transmission System from the protection system of the User's transformer, with the exception of the Transmission System Owner.

---

### T1.7.3 Generators

**T1.7.3.1** The specifications established in this part of the Rules apply to Generation Units to be connected to the Transmission System, with the exception of RES Units for which the relevant provisions of T16 apply. Regarding CHP Units, all relevant requirements apply, unless specified otherwise in the Connection Agreement.

It is recognized that Generation Units already connected to the Transmission System may have been designed based on different specifications and consequently may not comply in whole or in part with some or all of these specifications. In such cases, it is possible for them to be exempted from their respective obligations, following the prescribed procedure under the Transmission Rules.

**T1.7.3.1.1** Generation Units must satisfy at least the following specifications:

*(a)* Be capable of continuous operation at normal rated active power for Transmission System Frequencies ranging from 49.8 Hz to 50.2 Hz.

<!-- PAGE 59 -->

**Version 1.1.0** | **T1 – Connection Conditions to the Transmission System** | **Page 59**

---

(β) Remain synchronized to the Transmission System in case of Transmission System Frequency variation between 47.5 Hz and 52.0 Hz for a duration of at least 60 minutes.

(γ) Remain synchronized to the Transmission System in case of Transmission System Frequency variation between 47.0 Hz and 47.5 Hz for a duration of at least 10 seconds, in the event of a Frequency drop below 47.5 Hz.

(δ) Remain synchronized to the Transmission System in case a Rate of Change of Frequency (RoCoF) less than or equal to 1.0 Hz per second is observed, as measured over a rolling time window of 500 ms.

(ε) Remain synchronized to the Transmission System at normal active power operation for Transmission System Voltage within the range specified in T1.8.3.1 for step changes in Transmission System Voltage up to 10%.

(στ) Remain in operation with Reactive Power supply capability as specified in T1.7.3.6 at Transmission System Voltages within the range specified in T1.8.3.1, unless specified otherwise.

(ζ) Remain synchronized during Voltage dips on the High Voltage side of the Generation Unit Transformer, up to 95% of nominal Voltage (retaining up to 5% of nominal Voltage) for a duration of 0.2 seconds, and Voltage dips of the order of 50% of nominal Voltage (retaining up to 50% of nominal Voltage) for a duration of 0.6 seconds.

(η) Remain synchronized to the Transmission System during maximum load unbalance with negative sequence component as specified in standard IEC 60034-1 edition 12.0 or its amendments and/or other relevant IEC standards.

(θ) The short-circuit ratio of each Generation Unit must comply with standard IEC 60034-1 edition 12.0 or its amendments and/or other relevant IEC standards.

(ι) Have a Minimum Generation that shall not be greater than 50% of the Generation Capacity.

(ια) Have a Active Power Ramp-Up Rate that shall not be less than 1.5% of the Generation Capacity per minute when the Generation Unit has Started.

(ιβ) Have an Active Power Ramp-Down Rate that shall not be less than 1.5% of the Generation Capacity per minute when the Generation Unit has Started.

(ιγ) Have a Minimum Run Time after Start that shall not be greater than 6 hours for thermal Generation Units.

(ιδ) Have a Minimum Down Time that shall not be greater than 6 hours for thermal Generation Units.

(ιε) Have up to two prohibited operating zones between the minimum normal load and the Generation Capacity, and each of these must not be greater than 10% of the Generation Capacity.

(ιστ) Have a Load during Synchronization that shall not be greater than 10% of the Generation Capacity.

(ιζ) Have no-load operating time prior to transitioning to a longer-duration standby state, so as to remain in a hot standby state for at least 12 hours and in a warm standby state for at least 60 hours.

(ιη) Have a time to Synchronize (following Instruction):
* i. from hot standby state: not greater than 3 hours

<!-- PAGE 60 -->

**Version 1.1.0** | **T1 – Terms and Conditions for Connection to the Transmission System**  
**Page 60**

---

  ii. from warm state: not greater than 8 hours  
  iii. from cold state: not greater than 12 hours  

(s) [ιθ] To have a time from Synchronization State to the End of the Start-up Period:  
  i. from hot state: not greater than 40 minutes  
  ii. from warm state: not greater than 90 minutes  
  iii. from cold state: not greater than 180 minutes  

(t) [κ] Capability to execute Dispatch Instructions issued by TSOC within the framework of the Balancing Market, for any Generation level from Minimum Generation to Maximum Continuous Loading, in accordance with the relevant requirements of T5 and T15.  

(u) [κα] Capability to operate with an Automatic Generation Control (AGC) system.  

(v) [κβ] Automatic response of a Generating Unit to Frequency variation. The variation in Active Power generation due to the automatic response of the Unit to a Frequency variation shall satisfy the requirements specified in the Operating Reserve Policy.  

(w) [κγ] The Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) may require Generating Units with a Generation Capacity greater than or equal to 50 MW to be capable of operating with an Active Power Tele-control System for all loadings between the Minimum Tele-controlled Load and the Maximum Tele-controlled Load.  

---

### T1.7.3.1.2 
Users must install speed governors on Generating Units that comply with T5.3.4.4. Users are not permitted to alter the speed governor settings of a Generating Unit concerning frequency or load control without the written agreement of the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus.

---

### T1.7.3.1.3 
In the event of an exemption from the provisions of T1.7.3.1.1, Generating Units must operate in accordance with their Registered Operating Characteristics, so as to ensure maximum operational flexibility in a manner compatible with their type and construction and in accordance with Good Utility Practice rules. Where required, Operating Characteristics, and in particular Start-up times, shall be recorded separately for normal (scheduled) Start-up and for Start-up under emergency conditions, such as in the case of Start-up following the loss of a Generating Unit. Each Generator is obliged to implement operational procedures and practices that ensure immediate response to Instructions in accordance with the technical capabilities of the Generation Facilities.

---

### T1.7.3.1.4 
When the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus approaches a Generator, the Generator shall cooperate with the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus to establish operating procedures and methods that improve the response of each Generating Unit under emergency conditions, including, for example, the automatic Start-up of fast-start Generating Units following the loss of one or more Generating Units or when a loss of one or more Generating Units is anticipated. In all cases, confirmation from the Generator is required stating that these procedures are consistent with the safe operation of its Generation Facilities, and the Generator cannot unreasonably withhold such confirmation.

<!-- PAGE 61 -->

**T1 – Conditions of Connection to the Transmission System**
*Version 1.1.0 | Page 61*

***

**T1.7.3.2** When the Start-up time of a Generating Unit exceeds thirty minutes, suitable design shall be in place so that it possesses the capability, upon disconnection from the Transmission System, to curtail its generation in order to supply only the Auxiliary Loads of the Unit and maintain its operation (for example, supplying power solely to the Generating Unit Auxiliaries).

**T1.7.3.3** Synchronization Operations are performed by Producers using circuit breakers designated by the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC), which, depending on the configuration of the Facilities, include:
*   (a) the circuit breaker of the Generating Unit
*   (b) the Low Voltage and High Voltage circuit breakers of the Generating Unit Transformer.

The Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) provides the Producer with operational signals from its Facilities and Equipment as required to achieve synchronization at the High Voltage circuit breaker of the Generating Unit Transformer, in accordance with the relevant terms of the Connection Agreement.

**T1.7.3.4** The Synchronization arrangements specified in T1.7.3.3 must facilitate Synchronization under the following conditions:
*   (a) the Transmission System Frequency is between 48.0 and 52.0 Hz
*   (b) the Transmission System Voltage is within the limits specified in T1.8.3.1, without excluding the limits set in T1.7.3.6.

**T1.7.3.5** Each Generating Unit shall be designed, where feasible, in such a manner as to limit the risk of losing other Generating Units due to faults resulting from common operational states. In particular, each Generating Unit must be designed to be capable of operating with its primary Auxiliaries supplied via the Unit Auxiliary Transformer connected between the Generating Unit and the Low Voltage terminal busbars of the Generating Unit Transformer, or from another backup source as specified in agreement with the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC). Auxiliary supplies may be fed from alternative sources during delivery, Commissioning, Start-up, or emergency operating conditions, provided this complies with Good Industry Practice. In the case of Combined Cycle Generating Units, this rule applies only to steam turbine units.

**T1.7.3.6 Reactive Power Supply Capability**

**T1.7.3.6.1** Each Generating Unit must possess the following Reactive Power supply capability, measured at the generator terminal busbars:

<!-- PAGE 62 -->

**Edition 1.1.0**  
**T1 – Conditions of Connection to the Transmission System**  
**Page 62**

---

### Table 1.3: Reactive Power Capability

| Voltage Range | Connection Voltage Level | Range at Maximum Continuous Rating |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| 60 kV to 72 kV | 66 kV | Power factor from 0.93 capacitive to 0.80 inductive |
| 51 kV to 60 kV | 66 kV | Power factor from 1.00 to 0.80 inductive |
| 120 kV to 145 kV | 132 kV | Power factor from 0.93 capacitive to 0.80 inductive |
| 114 kV to 120 kV | 132 kV | Power factor from 1.00 to 0.80 inductive |
| 200 kV to 245 kV | 220 kV | Power factor from 0.93 capacitive to 0.80 inductive |
| 190 kV to 200 kV | 220 kV | Power factor from 1.00 to 0.80 inductive |

---

When Generating Units operate with active power output lower than the Maximum Continuous Rating, they must be capable of operating at every possible operating point within the generator P-Q capability diagram of said Generating Unit, down to at least the Minimum Generation level.

**T1.7.3.6.2** The Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) may require higher reactive power capability in the Transmission System than that specified in T1.7.3.6.1 for specific Generating Units. In such cases, the Producer must consult with the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) in order to satisfy these requirements.

**T1.7.3.6.3** The Generating Unit Transformer must have reactive power capability across the entire Voltage range of the Transmission System (as defined in T1.7.3.6.1).

**T1.7.3.6.4** The Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) and the Producer shall cooperate during the design phase to resolve any issues arising from the application of T1.7.3.6.

**T1.7.3.7** Every Generating Unit must be fitted with a fast proportional speed governor and load control unit or an equivalent control device, which allows frequency response under normal operating conditions in accordance with T2. The design and operation of the governor must be carried out in accordance with:
* (a) European Standards, or
* (b) in the absence of relevant European Standards, other relevant standards generally applied in European Union countries, which are in force as of the design date of the installation of which it forms a part. The typical governor droop setting ranges between 3% and 5%.

**T1.7.3.8** All Generating Units must be capable of participating in Voltage Regulation of the Transmission System by continuously regulating the Generating Unit Voltage through a suitable Automatic Voltage Regulator (AVR), which complies with applicable standards or equivalent European Standards and characteristics approved by the...

<!-- PAGE 63 -->

**Edition 1.1.0 | T1 – Terms of Connection to the Transmission System**
**Page 63**

...Transmission System Operator of Cyprus prior to the Connection Date, which cannot be unreasonably withheld.

**T1.7.3.9** Each Generating Unit Transformer must be equipped with an on-load tap changer system. The tap step adjustment shall not alter the Voltage ratio at the High Voltage terminal busbars by more than:
(a) 2.5% in the section of the Transmission System operating at 66 kV
(b) 1.6% in the section of the Transmission System operating at 132 kV and 220 kV unless a different relevant agreement exists with the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus.

---

### T1.8 TRANSMISSION SYSTEM OPERATING SPECIFICATIONS

**T1.8.1** The Transmission System Operator of Cyprus must, in accordance with the rules of Good Utility Practice, ensure that the Transmission System meets the technical, design, and operational specifications in relation to the operational performance targets of the system at the Connection Point, as specified in T1.8.

#### T1.8.2 Transmission System Frequency

**T1.8.2.1** The nominal Frequency of the Transmission System is defined at 50 Hz:
(a) Normal operation range: 49.8 to 50.2 Hz.
(b) During disturbances in the Transmission System: 47.0 to 52.0 Hz.

**T1.8.2.2** The Target Frequency is determined by the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus, in accordance with its reasonable judgment. The value of the Target Frequency is usually equal to 50 Hz. The Target Frequency is communicated by the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus to Users in accordance with T15A.3.3.1.

**T1.8.2.3** For reasons of safe operation of the Transmission System, the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus determines the Limit Rate of Change of Frequency (RoCoF) in the Transmission System under disturbance conditions, applying the applicable Policy for Determining the Limit Rate of Change of Frequency of the TSOC.

#### T1.8.3 Transmission System Voltages

**T1.8.3.1** The nominal Voltages of the Transmission System are defined at 220 kV, 132 kV, and 66 kV. The normal operating range is defined as ±10% of the nominal value in the 132 kV or 220 kV Transmission System and ±6% of the nominal value in the 66 kV Transmission System.

**T1.8.4** The negative sequence component of the phase Voltage of the Transmission System Voltages generally shall not exceed 5% under normal operating conditions.

**T1.8.5** The Transmission System features an effectively earthed neutral system, with an Earth Fault Factor of less than 1.4.

**T1.8.6** The Transmission System is designed and operated to maintain short-circuit levels indicatively below the following limits, for a maximum time duration of 1s:

<!-- PAGE 64 -->

**Version 1.1.0 | T1 – Connection Terms to the Transmission System | Page 64**

(a) in the section of the Transmission System operating at 132 kV: 31.5 kA. At specific points (where required by Generation and Transmission System conditions), higher limits are specified by TSOC on a case-by-case basis in the Connection Agreement.
(b) in the section of the Transmission System operating at 66 kV: 25 kA. At specific points, where required by Transmission System conditions, higher limits are specified by TSOC on a case-by-case basis in the Connection Agreement.

**T1.8.7** Further operational specifications regarding the Operational Security Limits of the Transmission System are defined by TSOC on a case-by-case basis.

---

### T1.9 MEASUREMENTS

**T1.9.1** Metering Arrangements are installed in accordance with the terms provided in the Connection Agreement and the specifications defined in T13.

---

### T1.10 PROTECTION AND POWER QUALITY OF USERS

Clause T1.10 applies to the Distribution System Operator, Producers, Energy Storage Facility Operators, and Consumers connected to the Transmission System.

**T1.10.1** Each User shall, in accordance with the rules of Good Utility Practice and to the extent reasonably practicable, ensure that any faults in their Facilities and Machinery cause the minimum possible disturbance to the Power System. Faults in Facilities and/or Machinery connected to the Transmission System shall be cleared as early as possible, without intentional time delay, and in any case the maximum clearing time is defined as:

(a) 120 ms for the Transmission System operating at 220 kV and 132 kV.
(b) For the Transmission System operating at 66 kV, minimum clearing times shall be determined by relevant stability studies if a need arises.

These maximum values are examined in stability analysis studies and, in some cases, different values may be specified in accordance with the terms of the Connection Agreement.

The above clearing times pertain only to primary protection systems. Without limiting this obligation, the User shall, at the latest by the connection of the User's System to the Transmission System, install and maintain, in accordance with the rules of Good Utility Practice, the protection equipment specified in T1.10.8 and T1.10.9.

**T1.10.2** For the avoidance of doubt, the adequacy of protection equipment installed by the User to protect User Facilities and Machinery against Transmission System disturbances shall be defined by the User. It should also be noted that the specifications of the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) primarily aim to protect the facilities of the Transmission System. Although these specifications are capable of providing a level of protection...

<!-- PAGE 65 -->

Version 1.1.0 T1 – Terms of Connection to the Transmission System  
Page 65

---

...to Users, were not originally designed to protect Users' installations.

**T1.10.2.1** The User, subject to prior approval by the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC), shall install protection devices as described in T1.10.2.

**T1.10.3** To satisfy T1.10.1, the minimum protection requirements for the installations of each User connected to the Transmission System may vary depending on the type, size, earthing arrangement, and connection method. The User protection systems required by the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) must be maintained in operation throughout the entire operating period of the connected installation.

**T1.10.4** It should be noted that automatic reclosing is an operational feature of the Transmission System, characterized by the restoration of power supply after a dead time. All tripping and restoration actions on the Transmission System are three-pole.

**T1.10.5** Users are recommended to take precautions against disturbances occurring on the Transmission System and, in particular, to install, without limitation, protection devices against:
* (a) load imbalance (negative phase sequence)
* (b) Overvoltages or Undervoltages
* (c) Overfrequency or Underfrequency
* (d) combination of cases (b) and (c), or operation at different tap changer positions of transformers connected in parallel, which may lead to overexcitation
* (e) automatic reclosure, where applicable.

Users may consult the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) regarding the suitability of the protection scheme they select.

**T1.10.6** To ensure the safe operation of the Transmission System, proper coordination, and fault identification across the Transmission System, the Distribution System, and Users' Systems, the protection system settings of Users that may have Operational Impacts on the Transmission System shall be notified to the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC). The Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) can and must prohibit User protection settings within a specific range of values. The protection systems to which these restrictions apply include, but are not limited to:
* (a) Underfrequency, overcurrent, or distance protection of Power Generating Modules or Electricity Storage Facilities
* (b) overexcitation, overcurrent, or distance protection of transformers
* (c) loss of main supply protection.

The notification process and, where applicable, the approval, determination, and modification of the above settings, are specified in the User's Connection Agreement or other specific agreements.

<!-- PAGE 66 -->

**Version 1.1.0** | **T1 – Terms of Connection to the Transmission System** | **Page 66**

---

**T1.10.7** The Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) shall provide the User with the necessary information and indications for the selective coordination and operation of the User's protection devices, in accordance with the relevant terms of the Connection Agreement, with other agreements, and with T1.7.2.5.4.

**T1.10.8.1** The Transmission System Owner must provide differential protection for Transformers connected to the Transmission System.

**T1.10.8.2** The Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) may require the Transmission System Owner to install supplementary protection devices when TSOC reasonably deems them necessary. Such devices include, but are not limited to, the following:
(a) directional overcurrent protection or distance protection on the transformer(s)
(b) protection against loss of main supply
(c) overvoltage or undervoltage protection
(d) underfrequency or overfrequency protection
(e) telecommunication channels between the two circuit breakers.

**T1.10.8.3** The Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) shall provide distance protection and/or overcurrent protection at each circuit breaker of the Connection Point with the Transmission System, unless otherwise agreed between TSOC and the Transmission System Owner.

---

### T1.10.9 Producers and Energy Storage Facility Operators

**T1.10.9.1** Producers and Energy Storage Facility Operators shall provide:
(a) **Differential protection** on the Generating Unit or Electricity Storage Facility Transformer. Connections between the circuit breaker at the Connection Point with the Transmission System and the High Voltage busbar terminals of the Generating Unit or Electricity Storage Facility Transformer must lie within the protected zone of the above differential protection.
(b) **Backup Protection** (of the Transmission System) at the Generating Units or Electricity Storage Facilities. The Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) reasonably requires the installation of one or more of the following protection systems: unit/facility overcurrent protection, unit/facility distance protection, or other special system.
(c) **Loss of excitation protection** (for Generating Units only).

**T1.10.9.2** The Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) may require an individual Producer, a group of Producers, an individual Energy Storage Facility Operator, or a group of Energy Storage Facility Operators to install supplementary protection and/or control devices, when TSOC reasonably deems them necessary. Such devices include, but are not limited to, the following:

<!-- PAGE 67 -->

(a) Overvoltage or undervoltage protection of the Generating Unit or Electricity Storage Facility.  
(b) Overfrequency protection of the Generating Unit or Electricity Storage Facility.  
(c) Loss of main supply protection (Rate of Change of Frequency - RoCoF or frequency vector shift).  
(d) Pole slipping protection (applicable to Generating Units only).  
(e) Direct intertripping of the circuit breaker at the Connection Point on the Transmission System and the circuit breaker at the User Connection Point.  

**T1.10.9.3** Distance protection towards the Generating Unit or the Electricity Storage Facility shall be provided by the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) at the circuit breaker of the Generating Unit or Electricity Storage Facility Transformer at the Connection Point on the Transmission System, so as to safeguard the Transmission System from the impacts of disturbances originating from Generating Units or Electricity Storage Facilities.  

---

### T1.10.10 Distribution System Operator

The Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) may require the Distribution System Operator (DSO) to install protection devices when TSOC reasonably deems them necessary. Such protection schemes include, but are not limited to, the following:

(a) Loss of main supply protection.  
(b) Undervoltage or overvoltage protection.  
(c) Underfrequency or overfrequency protection.  
(d) Differential protection on Distribution System lines or cables.  
(e) Distance protection at the circuit breaker of the User Connection Point with the Distribution lines or cables.  
(f) Telecommunication channels between the circuit breaker at the Connection Point on the Transmission System and the circuit breaker at the User Connection Point.  

---

### T1.10.11 Consumers Connected to the Transmission System

**T1.10.11.1** Consumers connected to the Transmission System shall provide differential protection for Transformers connected to the Transmission System.  

**T1.10.11.2** The Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) may require Consumers connected to the Transmission System to install supplementary protection devices when TSOC reasonably deems them necessary. Such protection schemes include the following:

(a) Directional overcurrent protection or distance protection for Transformers connected to the Transmission System, when the User's System includes Distributed Generation.  
(b) Loss of main supply protection, when the User's System includes Distributed Generation.  
(c) Undervoltage or overvoltage protection, when the User's System includes Distributed Generation.

<!-- PAGE 68 -->

**Version 1.1.0** | **T1 – Terms of Connection to the Transmission System** | **Page 68**

***

(d) Under-frequency or over-frequency protection, when the User's System includes Distributed Generation.

**T1.10.11.3** The Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) shall provide distance protection and/or overcurrent protection at each circuit breaker of the Connection Point to the Transmission System.

### T1.10.12 Power Quality

Users shall ensure that their connection to the Transmission System does not cause a disturbance or fluctuation in the supply Voltage on the Transmission System at the Connection Point exceeding the relevant limits agreed upon with the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC). Disturbance or fluctuation limits must fall within the range of values specified in the latest IEC standards for harmonics and Voltage fluctuation.

**T1.10.12.1** Every Consumer connected to the Transmission System shall ensure that for any loading exceeding 50% of the Maximum Import Capacity, the Average Total Power Factor as determined at the Connection Point for each half-hour period remains within the range of 0.92 lagging to 1. The Average Total Power Factor of a Consumer is calculated using the following equation:

$$\text{TPF} = \frac{\sum P}{\left[\left(\sum P\right)^2 + \left(\sum Q\right)^2\right]^{0.5}}$$

Where:
* **TPF** is the Average Total Power Factor of a Consumer
* **$\sum P$** is the Active Energy supplied to the Consumer at their Connection Point during any half-hour time interval
* **$\sum Q$** is the Reactive Energy supplied to the Consumer at their Connection Point during the same half-hour time interval

**T1.10.12.2** Every Consumer connected to the Transmission System who utilizes shunt capacitors shall maintain their impact on audio frequency signals (ripple control) used for load management within acceptable limits in accordance with instructions from the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC). The TSOC's instructions shall be based on advice from the Transmission System Owner regarding measures to be taken by Consumers connected to the Transmission System to minimize potential impacts on audio frequency signals.

---

### T1.11 TELECOMMUNICATION FACILITIES

**T1.11.1** Users are required to provide telecommunication facilities as specified in other sections of the Transmission Rules.

---

### T1.12 SIGNALS PROVIDED BY USERS

**T1.12.1** Every User shall provide signals and indications regarding the User's Facilities and Equipment as required by the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) in accordance with the Connection Agreement.

Users represented by Independent Aggregators in the Electricity Market may be exempted from specific provisions of T1.12, at the reasonable discretion of the TSOC.

<!-- PAGE 69 -->

| Version 1.1.0 | T1 – Transmission System Connection Conditions | Page 69 |
| :--- | :--- | ---: |

**T1.12.2** The signals and indications that Users are required to provide include, but are not limited to, the following categories (as applicable, depending on the case):
(a) Positions of protection devices and switching apparatus
(b) Measurements of energy, Voltage, and, at the discretion of TSOC, Frequency
(c) Indication of transformer tap position
(d) Fault indications and alarms
(e) Data collection for Incidents
(f) Indication of stored Energy
(g) Indications regarding operation in FSM, LFSM-O, and LFSM-U

**T1.12.3** The provision of signals and indications required to be provided by Users to the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus shall be achieved via telecommunication connection and installation of a Remote Terminal Unit (RTU) for continuous and uninterrupted transmission of data (e.g., circuit breaker status indication, Active and Reactive Power measurements, and other) from the User to the National Energy Control Centre of the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus, in accordance with the provisions of T8.2.

**T1.12.4** If the signals or indications required to be provided by the User in accordance with T1.12.2 are unavailable or non-compliant with the specified specifications due to technical equipment failure of the Users or for another cause controlled by the User, the User shall, in accordance with the rules of Good Industry Practice, restore or correct the signals and/or indications immediately.

**T1.12.5** Details regarding the presentation of signals provided by the User are specified by the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus in accordance with the Connection Agreement.

**T1.12.6** When the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus determines with justification that due to a Modification to the Transmission System or in order to satisfy a Transmission System requirement, supplementary signals and/or indications relating to the User's Facilities and Machinery are required, the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus shall inform the User accordingly. Taking into account this notification, the User shall ensure that the supplementary signals and/or indications are made available.

### T1.13 POWER SUPPLIES

**T1.13.1** Each User shall provide power at 400 V AC / 230 V AC in accordance with the requirements of the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus for the Transmission Substation facilities. The Equipment Loading Capacity and specific details are determined by the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus and are included in the User's Connection Agreement.

### T1.14 SAFETY RESPONSIBILITY

**T1.14.1** For each User Installation Site, Site Operating Regulations shall be prepared.

**T1.14.2** The Site Operating Regulations define in detail the boundary limits of responsibility for the safety of personnel performing work or Testing

<!-- PAGE 70 -->

Edition 1.1.0 | T1 – Connection Terms to the Transmission System | Page 70

...at the User Connection Site and in circuits passing through the User Installation Site at any point.

**T1.14.3** More detailed information regarding the procedures and responsibilities involved in safety procedures is set out in T12.

**T1.14.4** Producers or Storage Facility Operators for whom the Connection Agreement is signed with the TSOC, shall carry out the Tests and take the required actions to secure approval of their electrical installations and submit to the TSOC, prior to their connection, the Electrical Installation Inspection Certificate, in accordance with the provisions of T12.4.5.

### T1.15 EQUIPMENT TESTING, COMMISSIONING, AND NOTIFICATION

**T1.15.1** The Transmission System Operator of Cyprus and the User shall, in accordance with the terms set out in the Connection Agreement, define the Equipment Testing and Commissioning, which includes Equipment Testing and Commissioning Tests and Operational Tests of Equipment Connected to the Transmission System. The User's duties in relation to the Tests set out in T1.15 are additional requirements to those of the Connection Agreement.

**T1.15.2** Users shall conduct, as required, the necessary tests (defined in the Operational Tests of Equipment Connected to the Transmission System), confirming that the User's Facilities and Machinery operate in accordance with the requirements of the Transmission Rules, which must be fulfilled prior to the Operational Date. The Transmission System Operator of Cyprus may, in accordance with the Connection Agreement, notify the User of the Operational Tests of Equipment Connected to the Transmission System required to be conducted by the User. The Transmission System Operator of Cyprus may not perform tests regarding (a), (b), (c), (d), and (e) of T1.7.3.1.1, but retains the right to conduct a test to achieve compatibility between design and operation. For the avoidance of doubt, it is the responsibility of Users to continuously ensure compliance with the Transmission Rules and the success of the Tests.

**T1.15.3** Equipment Testing and Commissioning includes:
(a) Energization and De-energization requirements of part of the User System for conducting Tests (tests of circuit breaker operating mechanism, protection tests, etc.);
(b) Special Generation Schedule for Testing purposes.

The User shall immediately inform the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus regarding the above requirements, which include details on the type and duration of the required Tests. Users shall provide timely notice to the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (not less than fifteen (15) Business Days) prior to the execution date of the Equipment Testing and Commissioning Tests. The time and date of conducting these tests must be confirmed at least three (3) Business Days prior to carrying out these tests. If, following confirmation, the User reasonably considers that the tests must...

<!-- PAGE 71 -->

**T1 – Terms of Connection to the Transmission System** | Version 1.1.0 | Page 71

---

...be conducted before or after the agreed time and date, it must notify the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) within a reasonable timeframe regarding the rescheduling of the tests. The User shall immediately inform the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) regarding any significant change in the test requirements and details.

**T1.15.4** The information provided in T1.15.3 is for indicative purposes only and the User must, consequently, formally request from the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) the Equipment Control and Commissioning Test that requires Dispatching in accordance with the subsequent terms of T1.15, and no such Equipment Control and Commissioning Test shall be executed unless it receives a Dispatching Instruction in accordance with T1.15.

**T1.15.5** Users must submit a Written Application to the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) for conducting an Equipment Control and Commissioning Test requiring the issuance of an Instruction, pursuant to T1.15.4. This application shall include the following information:

**T1.15.5.1** Details regarding the proposed Equipment Control and Commissioning Test.

**T1.15.5.2** A proposal for the Special Generation Schedule is required from Users, when deemed necessary, for the completion of the Equipment Control and Commissioning Test, including the period of validity of the Instruction. When the User does not know in advance all the Instructions required for the completion of the Test, which become known after the completion of a portion of the Test, the User shall in the Test proposal:

(a) properly divide the Equipment Control and Commissioning Test into individual sections,

(b) indicate and negotiate which sections of the Equipment Control and Commissioning Test can be completed incrementally and which cannot,

(c) indicate potential deviations of the Equipment Control and Commissioning Test for the sections that can be completed incrementally.

Additionally, factors that may affect the completion of the sections must be communicated to the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) (specifically, the procedure to be followed when a specific section depends on the result of the preceding one).

**T1.15.5.3** A proposal regarding the preferred time or time intervals for the Equipment Control and Commissioning Test.

**T1.15.5.4** The time limits of the individual sections of the Equipment Control and Commissioning Test (if any) that can be completed independently and/or do not need to be repeated in the event of an interruption of the Equipment Control and Commissioning Test by the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) after the completion of a given section.

**T1.15.6** Producers and Storage Facility Operators (or Storage Representatives where applicable) shall be subject to the relevant provisions of the Electricity Market Rules as well as the provisions of Chapters T8 and T15 at least seven (7) days prior to the Operational Date and the Generation Unit or Storage Facility...

<!-- PAGE 72 -->

| Version 1.1.0 | T1 – Transmission System Connection Conditions | Page 72 |
| :--- | :--- | ---: |

...Electricity will be available for Dispatch from the Operational Date.

**T1.15.7** A User for whom Equipment Commissioning Tests and/or Operational Tests of Equipment Connected to the Transmission System are being performed, shall enter into a Commissioning Status as defined in the Electricity Market Rules, and all relevant provisions shall apply, including those of the Transmission Code and particularly those concerning the submission of a Special Generation Schedule pursuant to T9.

**T1.15.8** From the day following the Connection Date and in any event prior to the Operational Date, Users are required to verify (by providing to the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) the responses reasonably required, which include the results of the relevant Equipment Commissioning Tests or Operational Tests of Equipment Connected to the Transmission System) the technical data provided in accordance with the Planning Code, as well as other technical data reasonably required by the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) to be verified for assessing compliance with the Transmission Code or the Connection Agreement.

**T1.15.9** The values confirmed or verified in accordance with T1.15 must be included in the User's Registered Operational Characteristics and in the Registered Data.

### T1.16 OPERATING DIAGRAMS

#### T1.16.1 Description

**T1.16.1.1** An Operating Diagram shall be prepared for each Connection Site at which a Connection Point exists, utilizing, where necessary, the graphical symbols set out in Appendix A of T1.

**T1.16.1.2** The Operating Diagram must include all High Voltage Apparatus, connections to external circuits, and incorporate the numbering, nomenclature, and labeling. At Connection Sites where gas-insulated switchgear (GIS) and/or other High Voltage Apparatus with gas insulation media are installed, they shall be drawn within an area demarcated by a chain-dotted line intersecting the boundaries of the gas zones. The nomenclature used must comply with that used at the relevant Connection Sites and circuits. The Operating Diagram (and the schedule of technical details) is intended to provide an accurate record of the design and circuit interconnections, the Loading Capacity of the equipment, and the numbering and nomenclature of the High Voltage Apparatus and associated Plant/Facilities.

**T1.16.1.3** In the case of a User Site where the User is not the Transmission System Owner, the User shall prepare and submit to the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) an Operating Diagram for all High Voltage Apparatus located on the User's side of the Connection Point, and the Transmission System Operator shall provide to the User the Operating Diagram showing all High Voltage Apparatus located on the Owner's side...

<!-- PAGE 73 -->

**Version 1.1.0** | **T1 – Transmission System Connection Conditions** | **Page 73**

---

...of the Transmission System or another User at the Connection Point, in accordance with the time requirements of the Connection Agreement prior to the Works Completion Date.

**T1.16.1.4** The User shall prepare, produce, and distribute, using the information contained in the User's Operating Diagram, the Transmission System Owner's Operating Diagram, and the Installation Site Operating Diagram of any other User, a composite Operating Diagram for the complete Connection Site, in accordance with the time requirements of the Connection Agreement.

**T1.16.1.5** In the case where the Transmission System Owner's Installation Site is connected to the Transmission System, the User shall prepare, produce, and distribute the Operating Diagram for the complete Connection Site and all High Voltage Equipment, in accordance with the time requirements of the Connection Agreement.

**T1.16.1.6** In the case where the Transmission System Owner's Installation Site comes into contact with any other User's Installation Site, the User shall prepare and submit to the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) the Operating Diagram for all High Voltage Equipment at the connection point, and TSOC shall provide the User with an Operating Diagram for all High Voltage Equipment on the other User's side at the Connection Point, in accordance with the time requirements of the Connection Agreement prior to the Works Completion Date.

**T1.16.1.7** The User shall prepare, produce, and distribute, using the information contained in the User's Operating Diagram and the Installation Site Operating Diagram of another User, a composite Operating Diagram for the complete Connection Site, in accordance with the time requirements of the Connection Agreement.

### T1.16.2 Changes to the Operating Diagram

**T1.16.2.1** When the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) decides that it wishes to install new High Voltage Equipment or wishes to alter the existing numbering and nomenclature for certain High Voltage Equipment at the Transmission System Owner's Installation Site, the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) shall, one month prior to the change, send to the User a revised Operating Diagram of the Installation Site, incorporating the new High Voltage Equipment to be installed and their numbering and nomenclature or the changes, as applicable.

**T1.16.2.2** When the User decides that they wish to install new High Voltage Equipment or wish to alter the existing numbering and nomenclature for certain High Voltage Equipment at their Installation Site, the User shall (unless making a Modification pursuant to the Connection Agreement, where the provisions of the Connection Agreement regarding required notice times apply), one month prior to the installation or alteration, send to the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) a revised Operating Diagram of the User's Installation Site, incorporating the new High Voltage Equipment...

<!-- PAGE 74 -->

...of the User to be installed and their numbering and nomenclature or changes, as applicable.

---

### **T1.16.3 VALIDITY OF OPERATING DIAGRAMS**

The composite Operating Diagram prepared by the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) or by the User, as the case may be, shall be the final Operating Diagram for all operational and design activities related to the Connection Site. If a dispute arises regarding the accuracy of the composite Operating Diagram, a meeting shall be held immediately at the Connection Site, if reasonably practicable, between the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) and the User, to attempt to resolve the matters under dispute.

---

### **T1.16.4 COMMON SITE DRAWINGS**

#### **T1.16.4.1**
Common Site Drawings shall be prepared for each Connection Site and must include the Connection Site layout plans, electrical design drawings, common Protection / control drawings, and common services drawings.

#### **T1.16.4.2**
In the event where a Site of the User interfaces with any other Site of another User, the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) must submit to the User the Common Drawings of the adjacent Site (of the Transmission System or of another User's Site) in accordance with the time schedule requirements of the Connection Agreement. The User must prepare, produce, and distribute, using the information contained in the submitted Common Site Drawings of the Transmission System Owner or another User's Site, the Common Site Drawings for the integrated Connection Site in accordance with the time schedule requirements of the Connection Agreement.

#### **T1.16.4.3**
In the event where a Transmission System Owner Site interfaces with the Transmission System, the User must prepare and submit to the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) the Common Site Drawings in accordance with the time schedule requirements of the Connection Agreement.

#### **T1.16.4.4**
When the User becomes aware that it is necessary to modify any part of the Common Site Drawings of a Connection Site, the User must, as soon as reasonably practicable, prepare, produce, and distribute the revised Common Site Drawings for the integrated Connection Site.

If, in the reasonable opinion of the User, the modification can be addressed by Written notification to the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) of the change and the corresponding modification of the copy of the Common Site Drawings held by each party (or where only a single copy exists, by modification by the party retaining it), the User must notify the change and each party shall execute the modifications. If the changes result in a Modification under the Connection Agreement, the provisions of the Connection Agreement regarding time schedule shall apply.

<!-- PAGE 75 -->

**Edition 1.1.0** | **T1 – Terms of Connection to the Transmission System** | **Page 75**

---

### T1.16.4.5 
When the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) becomes aware that it is necessary to alter any part of the Common Site Drawings of a Connection Site, it must:

**(a)** if it concerns a Site of the Transmission System Owner, as soon as reasonably practicable, prepare, produce, and distribute the revised Common Site Drawings for the complete Connection Site;

**(b)** if it concerns a User's Site other than the Site of the Transmission System Owner, as soon as reasonably practicable, prepare and submit to the User the revised Common Site Drawings on the Transmission System Owner's side at the Connection Point, and the User must, as soon as reasonably practicable, prepare, produce, and distribute, using the information contained in the submitted Common Site Drawings of the Transmission System Owner, the revised Common Site Drawings for the complete Connection Site.

If, in the reasonable opinion of the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC), the change can be addressed by Written Notice to the User of the change and the relevant amendment to the copy of the Common Site Drawings existing on each side (or, where only one copy exists, by an amendment from the side holding it), the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) must notify the change and each party shall execute the changes. If the changes result in a Modification in accordance with the Connection Agreement, the terms of the Connection Agreement regarding timeline shall apply.

### T1.16.5 VALIDITY OF COMMON SITE DRAWINGS
The Common Site Drawings for the complete Connection Site prepared by the User or by the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC), as the case may be, shall be the definitive Common Site Drawings for all operational and design activities related to the Connection Site. If a dispute arises regarding the accuracy of the Common Site Drawings, a meeting shall be held promptly at the Connection Site, as soon as reasonably practicable, between the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) and the User to endeavor to resolve the matters in dispute.

### T1.16.6 ACCESS
The terms relating to access to User Sites are specified in each Interface Agreement between the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) and each involved User.

---

### T1.17 CONNECTION

#### T1.17.1 
The terms relating to connection to the Transmission System are included in each Connection Agreement with a User and include the terms relating to the submission of information and reports concerning compliance with the relevant Transmission System Connection Terms for the specific User, the Safety Rules, the

<!-- PAGE 76 -->

**Version 1.1.0 | T1 – Connection Conditions to the Transmission System | Page 76**

---

... commissioning and equipment testing schedules, Operating Diagrams, and connection approval.

**T1.17.2** Prior to the Completion of Works Date, the following must be submitted to the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC):

(a) Updated Planning Code data, with values estimated for design purposes confirmed, or, where feasible, replaced by validated actual values, updated future estimates, and updated forecasts, in accordance with the requirements of the Planning Code;

(b) Details of Protection arrangements and settings;

(c) Copies of all Safety Rules and Local Safety Instructions applicable to User Sites which will be used for the joint TSOC / User interface point (which, pursuant to T12, must satisfy TSOC regarding Isolation and Earthing procedures);

(d) An Operating Diagram for all High Voltage Apparatus on the User's side at the Connection Point as described in T1.16;

(e) Name of the User Site;

(f) Written confirmation that Safety Coordinators acting on behalf of the User are authorized and competent to implement the provisions of T12;

(g) Information to assist TSOC in preparing Joint Site Location Plans;

(h) Generators are required to submit the Installation Inspection Certificate as described in T1.14.4;

(i) A list of telephone numbers, facsimile numbers, electronic mail addresses, and website addresses (if available) of Users;

(j) Any other information that TSOC may reasonably require.

**T1.17.3** Additionally, at the time the information pursuant to T1.17.2 (f) is provided, TSOC shall provide written confirmation to the User that the Safety Coordinators acting on behalf of TSOC are authorized and competent to implement the provisions of T12.

<End of T1>

<!-- PAGE 77 -->

**T1.A - ANNEX TO TRANSMISSION SYSTEM CONNECTION CONDITIONS – INFORMATION FOR SITE OPERATING REGULATIONS, OPERATING DIAGRAMS AND JOINT SITE PLANS**

### T1.A1 FORM, PRINCIPLES AND BASIC PROCEDURES USED FOR PREPARING SITE OPERATING REGULATIONS

#### T1.A1.1 BASIC PRINCIPLES

**T1.A1.1.1** In all Site Locations, the following Site Operating Regulations must be prepared using the attached draft or the modifications agreed between the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) and the Users, and in the absence of such agreement, the attached draft shall be used:

(a) High Voltage Apparatus Regulation.
(b) Low Voltage / Medium Voltage Installations, Apparatus, Services, and Power Supply Regulation.
(c) Telecommunications and Metering Equipment Regulation.

Except for Generating Units and Power Station sites, the regulations referred to in (b) and (c) must be combined.

**T1.A1.1.2** Each Site Operating Regulation for a Connection Site must be prepared by the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) following consultation with the other Users at least 2 weeks prior to the Completion Date of Works for the specific Connection Site (which may form part of the Site Location). Each User shall, in accordance with the time requirements of the Connection Agreement, provide information to the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) to enable it to prepare the Site Operating Regulation.

**T1.A1.1.3** Each Site Operating Regulation is subdivided into sections to account for each distinct Connection Site within a Site Location.

**T1.A1.1.4** Each Site Operating Regulation shall describe in detail each item of Plant and Apparatus as follows:

(a) Ownership of Plant / Apparatus,
(b) Operator of the Site Location (Controller),
(c) Safety (applicable Safety Rules and Control Persons or other responsible persons (Safety Coordinator), or other persons responsible for safety),
(d) Operations (applicable Operating Procedures and control engineer),
(e) Responsibility for maintenance execution.

Each Connection Point must be recorded with accuracy.

**T1.A1.1.5** In the case of Site Operating Regulations specified under T1.A1.1.1 (b) and (c), with the exception of the operation of Protection Devices, it is sufficient to indicate as responsible person/entity the User or the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC), as applicable. In the case of Site Operating Regulations specified under T1.A1.1.1 (a) for Protection Devices, the unit

---
*Version 1.1.0 | T1A – Transmission System Connection Conditions - Annexes | Page 77*

<!-- PAGE 78 -->

**Edition 1.1.0** | **T1A – Terms of Connection to the Transmission System - Appendices** | **Page 78**

---

...management entity responsible must be stated in addition to the User or the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC), as the case may be.

**T1.A1.1.6** The Site Operating Regulations for the High Voltage Apparatus of each Connection Site must include the lines and cables departing from the Connection Site.

**T1.A1.1.7** Every page of the Site Operating Regulations must contain the publication date and the issue number.

**T1.A1.1.8** When the Site Operating Regulations are prepared, they must be sent by the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) to the involved Users for confirmation of their accuracy.

**T1.A1.1.9** The Site Operating Regulations must be signed by the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) and, on behalf of each involved User, by the Authorized Representative (see T1.A1.1.16), providing written confirmation of their accuracy.

**T1.A1.1.10** Once signed, two copies shall be distributed by the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC), not less than two weeks prior to their implementation date, to each User who is a party to the Site Operating Regulations, accompanied by a note indicating the issue number and the implementation date.

**T1.A1.1.11** The Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) and the Users must make the Site Operating Regulations readily available to the operational personnel of the Site.

### T1.A1.1.12 Modifications to Existing Site Operating Regulations

Without prejudice to the provisions of T1.A1.1.15, when a User specified in the Site Operating Regulations becomes aware that a modification is necessary, they must inform the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) immediately and in any case 8 weeks prior to the implementation of any modification (or as soon as practicable after becoming aware of it, if less than 8 weeks remain from the moment the User becomes aware of this change).

**T1.A1.1.13** When the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) is notified of a change by the User, or proposes a change itself, it shall prepare a revised Site Operating Regulation within a time frame of not less than six weeks prior to the implementation of the change (provided that it has been notified or is aware of the change eight weeks prior to this time), and the procedures specified in T1.A1.1.8 must be followed in accordance with the revised Site Operating Regulation.

**T1.A1.1.14** The revised Site Operating Regulation must be signed in accordance with the procedure specified in T1.A1.1.9 and distributed in accordance with the procedure specified in T1.A1.1.10,

<!-- PAGE 79 -->

---
**Edition 1.1.0** | **T1A – Connection Terms to the Transmission System - Appendices** | **Page 79**
---

...accompanied by a note indicating at which point the modifications have been made, the publication number, and the date of implementation.

### T1.A1.1.15 
When a User listed in the Site Operating Regulations, or the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC), as the case may be, realizes that a modification of the Site Operating Regulations is urgently necessary to reflect, for example, an emergency situation, the User must notify TSOC, or TSOC must notify the User, as the case may be, immediately and they must discuss:

(a) what change is necessary to the Site Operating Regulations;
(b) whether the Site Operating Regulations are to be modified temporarily or permanently;
(c) the distribution of the revised Site Operating Regulations.

TSOC shall prepare a revised Site Operating Regulations as soon as possible, and in any event within seven (7) days from its notification or the notification of the necessary modifications. The Site Operating Regulations shall be confirmed by the Users and signed on behalf of TSOC and the Users (by the persons referred to in T1.A1.1.9) as soon as possible following its preparation and dispatch to the Users for confirmation.

---

### T1.A1.1.16 Authorized Representatives

Each User shall, prior to the Completion Date of Works, provide TSOC with a list of representatives who have been duly authorized to sign the Site Operating Regulations on behalf of the User ("Authorized Representative"). TSOC shall, prior to the Completion Date of Works, provide that User with information regarding the competent representatives of TSOC and their respective contact details. Each User must provide the other Users with any change to this list six (6) weeks prior to the implementation of such change when the change was anticipated, and as soon as possible after the change when the change was not anticipated.

---

### T1.A1.1.17 Decommissioning of Connection Sites

When a Connection Site is to be decommissioned, the User desiring the decommissioning procedure must contact TSOC to make arrangements regarding the modification of the Site Operating Regulations at the relevant point in time.
