# T6 - DEMAND CONTROL UNDER EMERGENCY CONDITIONS

## T6.1 INTRODUCTION

### T6.1.1
T6 deals with the measures taken by the Distribution System Operator and the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus for Consumers connected to the Transmission and Distribution System, which allow for Demand curtailment in the event that the electrical power produced/injected by available facilities (Generating Units or Electricity Storage Facilities) is insufficient to cover Demand, as well as in the event of a fault or other operational problems affecting Frequency, Voltage, or creating overloads relative to the thermal or other loading limits of any part of the Transmission System.

It is explicitly clarified that in cases of significant malfunction of the Transmission System or the Distribution System, or in the event that the available generation capacity is insufficient to cover Demand in real time, the Electricity Market Rules are suspended and the provisions hereof shall prevail.

### T6.1.2
T6 regulates the following:
(a) Consumer Demand Curtailment, following Instructions from the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus;
(b) Consumer Demand Reconnection, following Instructions from the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus;
(c) Automatic Under-Frequency Demand Shedding;
(d) Automatic Under-Voltage Demand Disconnection;
(e) Automatic Over-Voltage Demand Disconnection;
(f) Automatic Demand Re-energisation Following Frequency Recovery.

The term "Demand Control under Emergency Conditions" is used to describe any or all of the above methods for Demand reduction, as well as cases (b) and (f) which concern Demand increase.

### T6.1.3
When measures for Demand Control under Emergency Conditions are implemented, detailed reference shall be made to the Active Power associated with the curtailment or increase of Demand.

### T6.1.4
When the implementation of measures for Demand Control under Emergency Conditions is necessary, these shall not be applied, as far as possible, to Priority Supply Consumers. Therefore, by way of exception, T6 does not apply to these Consumers.

### T6.1.5
The implementation of measures for Demand Control under Emergency Conditions shall be carried out at the reasoned discretion of TSOC and DSO, among Consumers connected to the Transmission System and Consumers connected to the Distribution System.

### T6.1.6
For Demand Control under Emergency Conditions, the necessary switching operations are performed on the Distribution System and/or the Transmission System (in the case of Consumers connected to the Transmission System). Demand Control under Emergency Conditions relates to the physical

<!-- PAGE 156 -->

**Draft Version 1.1.0** | **T6 – Emergency Demand Control** | **Page 156**

---

...structure of the Electrical System and bears no relation to any existing contractual obligations. If in a specific area Emergency Demand Control is necessary, the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) shall proceed with Consumer Demand Shedding regardless of their Supplier, since owing to the diversity of contracts it is difficult to identify the involved parties, while even in cases where this is feasible, it is not certain that the desired result will be achieved.

**T6.1.7** When Emergency Demand Control is necessary, the implementation of the measures is carried out by the Distribution System Operator (DSO). However, Suppliers are required to highlight to their Consumers that they may be affected by Emergency Demand Control, even though they are not involved in the implementation of the relevant measures.

### T6.2 PURPOSE

**T6.2.1** The objective of T6 is to define the means made available to the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) by the Distribution System Operator (DSO) and Consumers connected to the Transmission System, so that TSOC has the capability of Demand Shedding in order to avoid or mitigate operational problems that may occur across the whole or a section of the Transmission System. By using these means, TSOC may issue Instructions for Emergency Demand Control in such a manner that non-discriminatory measures are applied neither against nor in favor of a User or a group of Users. Furthermore, it defines the method ensuring that TSOC is kept informed of cases where certain Users did not implement Emergency Demand Control measures in accordance with the Instructions of TSOC.

### T6.3 SCOPE OF APPLICATION

T6 applies to the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) and to the following, each of which is a User pursuant to T6:
(a) Distribution System Operator (DSO)
(b) Suppliers
(c) Consumers connected to the Transmission System

### T6.4 PROCEDURE FOR IMPLEMENTING EMERGENCY DEMAND CONTROL VIA INSTRUCTIONS OF THE TRANSMISSION SYSTEM OPERATOR – ROTATIONAL DEMAND SHEDDING

**T6.4.1** In the event that a deficiency in Capacity Adequacy or any other cause rendering the implementation of Emergency Demand Control necessary is foreseen, the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) shall warn the Distribution System Operator (DSO) by issuing a corresponding Alarm as specified in T10.

**T6.4.2** When the necessity for Emergency Demand Control becomes apparent in due time, the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) shall put into effect the measures provided for in the Plan...

<!-- PAGE 157 -->

**Draft Version 1.1.0** | **T6 – Demand Control under Emergency Conditions**  
**Page 157**

---

...Rotational Demand Shedding. The Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) and the Distribution System Operator (DSO) have corresponding responsibilities for maintaining the appropriate procedures and shall cooperate for the implementation of Demand Control under Emergency Conditions in accordance with the Rotational Demand Shedding Scheme.

**T6.4.3** When the need for Demand Control under Emergency Conditions arises suddenly, for practical reasons measures may be implemented that are not in accordance with those provided for in the Rotational Demand Shedding Scheme. The Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) and the Distribution System Operator (DSO) shall maintain appropriate procedures (in the formulation of which they cooperate) for the immediate implementation of Demand Control under Emergency Conditions when necessary, in accordance with the Instructions issued by the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC).

**T6.4.4** In the event that the measures implemented for Demand Control under Emergency Conditions are not in accordance with those provided for in the Rotational Demand Shedding Scheme (due to the sudden emergence of the operational necessity for their implementation or for other reasons), and provided that their prolonged application is anticipated, the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) shall ensure that the measures specified in the Rotational Demand Shedding Scheme are implemented as soon as possible. The Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) may issue Instructions for the appropriate modification of the measures provided for in the Rotational Demand Shedding Scheme, such that during their implementation due consideration is given to those Consumers who bore the consequences of load shedding during the initial phase prior to the implementation of the measures provided for in the Scheduled Rotational Load Shedding.

**T6.4.5** The Rotational Demand Shedding Scheme provides for the disconnection and reconnection of specific quantities of Demand following Instructions issued by the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC). In this manner, the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) has the capability to issue Instructions for the disconnection (and reconnection) of the exact quantity of Demand required each time by operational circumstances. The Distribution System Operator (DSO) shall comply with the Instructions issued by the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC), executing the provisions of the Rotational Load Shedding Scheme, and specifically shall not re-energize Demand quantities that differ from those defined by the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) in its Instructions.

**T6.4.6** The Rotational Demand Shedding Scheme provides for the notification of Consumers by the Distribution System Operator (DSO), via Mass Media, regarding the expected duration of the implementation of Demand Control under Emergency Conditions and regarding the specific Consumer groups facing the highest probability of supply interruption during specific time periods.

**T6.4.7** The Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) and the Distribution System Operator (DSO) shall maintain records in which data regarding the disconnection and reconnection of Consumers during the implementation of the Rotational Demand Shedding Scheme will be logged.

<!-- PAGE 158 -->

*Draft Version 1.1.0* | *T6 – Demand Management in Emergency State* | *Page 158*

---

...Demand (including the cases referred to in T6.4.3).

**T6.4.8** The Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC), in consultation with the Distribution System Operator (DSO), may also utilize the Load Telecontrol / Ripple Control System for Demand shedding purposes.

**T6.4.9** Within the framework of Demand Management in Emergency State, the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) may implement measures, deemed appropriate at its discretion, regarding power absorption by Storage Systems.

---

### T6.5 AUTOMATIC UNDER-FREQUENCY LOAD SHEDDING (UFLS)

**T6.5.1** The Distribution System Operator (DSO) shall take the necessary measures to achieve Automatic Under-Frequency Load Shedding (UFLS) corresponding to a percentage of the total peak Consumer Demand, as specified by the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC), to mitigate the impact of a significant loss of Generation or another major event in the overall Power System that results in a Generation deficit in a section of the Power System. The Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) shall determine the Frequency thresholds and the corresponding Demand percentages subject to Automatic Under-Frequency Load Shedding (UFLS) whenever Frequency values breach these thresholds downwards.

**T6.5.2** The Demand of the Distribution System Operator (DSO) subject to Automatic Under-Frequency Load Shedding (UFLS) shall be divided into distinct Active Power groups. The number, location, size, and associated low-Frequency thresholds for each of these groups shall be specified by the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) following consultation with the Distribution System Operator (DSO) and shall be reviewed annually by the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC). The allocation of these groups shall be carried out in such a manner as to achieve uniform disconnection within the Distribution System for all Supply Points from the Transmission System.

**T6.5.3** Consumers connected to the Transmission System shall make provision for Automatic Under-Frequency Load Shedding (UFLS), which shall be divided into distinct steps. The number, size, and associated low-Frequency thresholds for each of these steps shall be specified by the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC), following consultation with Consumers connected to the Transmission System.

**T6.5.4** Within the framework of Automatic Under-Frequency Load Shedding (UFLS), the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) may implement measures, deemed appropriate at its discretion, regarding power absorption by Storage Systems.

---

### T6.6 AUTOMATIC DEMAND RESTORATION FOLLOWING FREQUENCY RECOVERY

**T6.6.1** The Distribution System Operator (DSO) shall take the necessary measures for Automatic Demand Restoration Following Frequency Recovery...

<!-- PAGE 159 -->

**Draft Version 1.1.0** | **T6 – Demand Control in Emergency State** | **Page 159**

***

...Frequency in a section or the entirety of the Demand that was subjected to Automatic Under-Frequency Load Shedding (UFLS). The Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) retains the right to determine the Frequency limits and the corresponding Demand percentages for which Automatic Demand Restoration occurs After Frequency Restoration. Particular attention must be given to the status of the Transmission System during the energization of High Voltage underground cables.

**T6.6.2** Following the application of Automatic Under-Frequency Load Shedding (UFLS), the Distribution System Operator (DSO) shall not restore power supply to Consumers until Instructions are issued by the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC), unless another procedure has been agreed upon.

**T6.6.3** If conditions do not allow the restoration, within a reasonable period of time, of a large percentage of the total Demand that has been Disconnected by Automatic Under-Frequency Load Shedding (UFLS), the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) may instruct the Distribution System Operator (DSO) to perform additional manual Demand Disconnection and restore supply to an equivalent section of Demand that was automatically Disconnected. The objective of this action is to ensure the mitigation of a new Frequency drop through the operation of Automatic Under-Frequency Load Shedding. If the need for Demand Control in Emergency State is anticipated to persist for a prolonged period of time, the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) shall implement the Rotational Load Shedding Plan, in accordance with T6.4.

**T6.6.4** If conditions are such that, following the application of Automatic Under-Frequency Load Shedding (UFLS), Frequency values remain higher or lower than nominal values or another relevant incident occurs, the further implementation of Demand Control measures in Emergency State may be deemed necessary, in accordance with T6.4.

**T6.6.5** As soon as the Frequency is restored, the Distribution System Operator (DSO) shall implement without delay the Instructions of the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) for reconnection and the agreed procedures for Demand restoration.

### T6.7 AUTOMATIC UNDER-VOLTAGE LOAD SHEDDING

**T6.7.1** The Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) may occasionally decide that it is necessary to implement Automatic Under-Voltage Load Shedding (UVLS) on Consumer Demand or on feeder lines of the Transmission System Owner, in order to limit the impacts of the loss of Generating Units or another incident on the Transmission System that results in Voltages in certain areas of the Transmission System falling outside the limits defined in the Connection Conditions to the Transmission System.

**T6.7.2** The Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) may implement Automatic Under-Voltage Load Shedding (UVLS) at the Transmission System level. However, depending on the required extent of UVLS, and in order to prevent disconnecting a larger amount of Consumer Demand than is actually required due to the occurrence of a specific...

<!-- PAGE 160 -->

**Draft Version 1.1.0**  
**T6 – Demand Management under Emergency Conditions**  
*Page 160*

***

...event or the overall status of the system, it is preferable that UVLS is applied at the Distribution System level (provided this is possible).

**T6.7.3** The Distribution System Operator shall cooperate with the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC), when the TSOC requests its cooperation, for the design and implementation of UVLS in sections of the Distribution System, when this method is indicated in accordance with T6.7.2. The TSOC retains the right to full control over the activation or deactivation of UVLS, as well as for defining the Voltage limits at which UVLS shall be applied in each case. Generally, the limits shall be defined by the TSOC following consultations with the Distribution System Operator, but the TSOC may alter these limits whenever necessary to take into account special conditions arising during a specific period. The Distribution System Operator shall respond to any change in the defined limits with a corresponding adjustment of the settings without delay.

### T6.8 AUTOMATIC DEMAND DISCONNECTION FROM OVERVOLTAGE

**T6.8.1** The TSOC may occasionally decide that it is necessary to implement Automatic Load Shedding from Overvoltage on feeder lines of the Transmission System Owner, in order to ensure that Voltages in certain areas of the Transmission System do not fall outside the limits defined in the Transmission System Connection Conditions and to minimize equipment damage.

**T6.8.2** The Distribution System Operator shall cooperate with the TSOC, when the TSOC requests its cooperation, for the design and implementation of Automatic Demand Disconnection from Overvoltage (OVLS) in sections of the Distribution System, including shunt capacitor banks where necessary. The TSOC retains the right to full control over the activation or deactivation of OVLS, as well as for defining the Voltage limits at which OVLS shall be applied in each case. Generally, the limits shall be defined by the TSOC following consultations with the Distribution System Operator, but the TSOC may alter these limits whenever necessary to take into account special conditions arising during a specific period. The Distribution System Operator shall respond to any change in the defined limits with a corresponding adjustment of the settings without delay.

\<End of T6\>

## T7 - PROVISIONS FOR INDEPENDENT AGGREGATORS

### Version 1.1.0

Version 1.1.0 | T7 – Provisions for Independent Aggregators | Page 162

# T7 – PROVISIONS FOR INDEPENDENT AGGREGATORS

## T7.1 INTRODUCTION

**T7.1.1** In order for the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) to be able to fulfill its statutory obligations within the framework of the operation of the Electricity Market, it is necessary to establish specific provisions regarding Independent Aggregators.

## T7.2 PURPOSE

**T7.2.1** The provisions of T7 apply specifically to Independent Aggregators within the framework of their participation in the Electricity Market and the safe and reliable operation of the Power System. More specifically, they include, without being limited to, provisions regarding:

* (a) Requirements concerning the participation of Users in the Electricity Market;
* (b) Technical specifications concerning Generation/Demand Entities represented by Users;
* (c) Communication requirements of Users with the TSOC;
* (d) Data provision requirements from Users.

## T7.3 SCOPE OF APPLICATION

**T7.3.1** The provisions of T7 apply to the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) and to the following Users:

* (a) RES and CHP Aggregators
* (b) Demand Response Providers
* (c) Storage Representatives

## T7.4 SPECIAL REQUIREMENTS

**T7.4.1** The provisions of T7.4 do not replace or override the obligations of any RES and CHP Producer, Consumer, or Storage Facility Operator, as these are specified in other parts of the TMC.

**T7.4.2** Each User, at the discretion of the TSOC, is obliged to:

* (a) Possess appropriate equipment so as to be capable of executing the Instructions received from the TSOC concerning each portfolio it represents within the framework of the Electricity Market operation.
* (b) Satisfy the requirements described in T7.4.
* (c) Comply with the provisions of T7.5 and T8.2 regarding communication and the signals provided to the TSOC.
* (d) Provide the TSOC with the data described in T7.6.

**T7.4.3** Each User is obliged to ensure that the following technical requirements are met by all units comprising the portfolio it represents:

* (a) Be capable of varying Active Power generation / injection / absorption in accordance with their Registered Operating Characteristics.

<!-- PAGE 163 -->

**Edition 1.1.0** | **T7 – Provisions for Independent Aggregators** | **Page 163**

---

(b) Remain in operation when the Frequency remains within the normal operation range specified in T1.8.2.1.  
(c) Remain in operation in the event of a Frequency variation between 47.5 Hz and 52 Hz for a duration of at least 60 minutes.  
(d) Remain in operation in the event of a Frequency variation between 47.0 Hz and 47.5 Hz for a duration of at least 10 seconds, in case of a Frequency drop below 47.5 Hz.  
(e) Remain in operation if a Rate of Change of Frequency (RoCoF) less than or equal to 1.0 Hz per second is observed, as measured over a rolling time window of 500 ms.  
(f) Remain in operation at a Voltage within the range specified in T1.8.3.1 for step changes of the Transmission System Voltage up to 10%. For connection cases to the Distribution System, the corresponding provisions of the Distribution Code shall apply.  
(g) Have a minimum Rate of Change as shall be defined by directive of TSOC.  

**T7.4.4** Each User is obligated to perform the appropriate Tests (in cooperation with TSOC or DSO as required) in order to verify the Registered Data submitted to TSOC, which may concern a portfolio as a whole or only part of a portfolio.  

### T7.5 PROVIDED SIGNALS AND INDICATIONS

**T7.5.1** Each User shall provide signals and indications regarding the Generation/Demand Entities it represents, as required by the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC).  

**T7.5.2** The signals and indications that Users are required to provide pertain to each portfolio as a whole that they represent and include, but are not limited to, the following (as applicable):  
(a) Operating status (e.g., available to receive Dispatch Instructions from TSOC)  
(b) Total energy and power measurements  
(c) Event data logging  
(d) Stored Energy indication (for Energy Storage Facility portfolios)  

**T7.5.3** The provision of signals and indications required to be supplied by Users to the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) shall be achieved via telecommunication connection and installation of a Remote Terminal Unit (RTU) for continuous and uninterrupted transmission of data (e.g., Active and Reactive Power measurements, stored Energy indication in Energy Storage Facilities, etc.) from the User to TSOC's SCADA/EMS system, in accordance with the provisions of T8.2.  

**T7.5.4** If the signals or indications required to be provided by the User are unavailable or non-compliant with the specified technical requirements due to equipment failure of the User or any other reason within the User's control, the User shall, in accordance with Good Utility Practice, immediately restore or rectify the signals and/or indications.

<!-- PAGE 164 -->

**Version 1.1.0 | T7 – Provisions for Independent Aggregators**  
**Page 164**

---

**T7.5.5** Details regarding the presentation of signals provided by the User are specified by the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC).

**T7.5.6** When the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) determines with reasoned justification that, due to a Modification to the Transmission System or in order to satisfy a requirement of the Transmission System, supplementary signals and/or indications related to the Generation/Demand Entities represented by a User are required, TSOC shall inform the User accordingly. Taking this notification into account, the User shall ensure that the supplementary signals and/or indications will be made available.

---

### **T7.6 DATA PROVISION**

**T7.6.1** Users are required to declare the data specified by the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) for each Generation / Demand Entity they represent. It is noted that the data below pertain to the portfolios as a whole and include, at a minimum, the following:

**i. For RES and CHP portfolios and for Active Demand (AD) Load portfolios:**

*   **(a)** The minimum and maximum Active Power generation / absorption capacity.
*   **(b)** The maximum Ramp Rate, both increasing and decreasing.
*   **(c)** The capability to provide Ancillary Services as follows:
    *   *i.* The maximum technical capability to provide Fast Frequency Response (FFR), Frequency Containment Reserve (FCR) (upward and downward), and Frequency Restoration Reserve (FRR) (upward and downward).
    *   *ii.* The capability to operate under Automatic Generation Control (AGC), and where such capability exists, the control range and the minimum upward and downward Ramp Rate of absorbed Active Power under AGC.
    *   *iii.* Any limitations regarding the provision of Ancillary Services.
*   **(d)** The start-up time, defined as the time from the moment the Generation / Demand Entity is not generating or absorbing energy until it reaches normal operating status.
*   **(e)** A document serving as evidence of the User's compliance with the requirements of the Market Rules. Through this document, the Registered Information of the portfolio must also be verified.

**ii. For Electricity Storage Facility portfolios:**

*   **(a)** Maximum Charging Capacity of the Electricity Storage Facility.
*   **(b)** Maximum Discharging Capacity of the Electricity Storage Facility.
*   **(c)** Minimum Available Energy Capacity of the Electricity Storage Facility.
*   **(d)** Maximum Available Energy Capacity of the Electricity Storage Facility.

<!-- PAGE 165 -->

**Version 1.1.0** | **T7 – Provisions for Independent Aggregators** | **Page 165**

---

(e) Maximum Ramp Rate  
(f) The capability to provide Ancillary Services as follows:  
$$\quad\text{i.}$$ The maximum technical capability to provide Fast Frequency Regulation Reserve, Frequency Containment Reserve (FCR) (upward and downward), and Frequency Restoration Reserve (FRR) (upward and downward).  
$$\quad\text{ii.}$$ The capability to operate under Automatic Generation Control (AGC), and where such capability exists, the control range and the minimum upward and downward Ramp Rate under AGC.  
$$\quad\text{iii.}$$ Any limitations regarding the provision of Ancillary Services.  
(g) A document constituting proof of compliance of the User with the requirements of the Market Rules. Through this document, the Registered Information of the portfolio must also be confirmed.  

### T7.6.2. Requirements for modeling

**T7.6.2.1** Users shall make available to the TSOC, for each portfolio they represent, without delay, static and dynamic models that simulate the behavior of their portfolios under normal and abnormal operating conditions. These models must be provided to the TSOC electronically in DigSILENT PowerFactory® format and comply with the criteria and requirements defined by the TSOC. The electronic file delivered must be compatible with the latest version of the DigSILENT PowerFactory® software. The integrated static and dynamic model submitted must include (without being limited to) at least the modeling of all relevant control systems.

\<End of T7\>

# T8 - INFORMATION EXCHANGE

## T8.1 NOTIFICATIONS OF INCIDENTS AND OPERATIONS

### T8.1.1 INTRODUCTION

**T8.1.1.1** T8.1 describes the requirements for information exchange regarding Operations and/or Incidents of a Power System, which had (or may have had) or will have (or may have) an Operational Impact and constitute Significant System Incidents on the:
*   (a) Transmission System, in the case of an Operation and/or Incident on a User System.
*   (b) User System, in the case of an Operation and/or Incident on the Transmission System.

**T8.1.1.2** The obligation for notification pursuant to T8.1 concerns the notification from a User to TSOC (or vice versa) regarding Operations and/or Incidents that have occurred or are about to occur.

**T8.1.1.3** The main information required by the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus for analyzing Significant System Incidents is collected as follows:
*   (a) Via the SCADA/EMS of the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus, the Dispatch Information Management System, or other Data Collection Systems.
*   (b) Information provided to the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus by Users in accordance with other provisions of the Transmission Rules.

**T8.1.1.4** To ensure that the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus receives as quickly as possible all the information required to operate the Transmission System, and to ensure that no information is lost, T8.1 provides a comprehensive set of requirements. Furthermore, it provides for the provision of information to Users, aiming to assist Users.

**T8.1.1.5** In the event that a User has an obligation under other provisions to provide data to the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus, they are not obliged to provide data under T8.1 as well. Indicatively, this applies to Unavailability Declarations (total or partial) submitted in accordance with the Electricity Market Rules, as well as to data notified via the Market Management System.

### T8.1.2 PURPOSE

The purpose of T8.1 is to define the information exchange such that the implications of an Operation and/or Incident, along with the potential risks arising from them, can be assessed and the necessary measures taken by the involved parties to maintain the integrity of the Power System.

### T8.1.3 SCOPE

<!-- PAGE 168 -->

**Edition 1.1.0** | **T8 – Information Exchange** | **Page 168**

---

**T8.1.3.1** T8.1 applies to the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) and the following Users:
* (a) **Producers**, for Generating Units connected to the Transmission System as well as for Generating Units connected to the Distribution System for which a Connection Agreement has been concluded with the TSOC;
* (b) **Storage Facility Operators** connected to the Transmission System;
* (c) **Distribution System Operator**;
* (d) **Consumers** connected to the Transmission System;
* (e) **Independent Aggregators**;
* (f) **Load Representatives**;
* (g) **Transmission System Owner**.

**T8.1.3.2** The Transmission System Operator of Cyprus and Users are obligated pursuant to T8.1 to take into account information received from third parties when making a decision to determine whether an Operation or Incident is about to occur or has already occurred.

---

### T8.1.4 NOTIFICATION OBLIGATION

**T8.1.4.1** Without limiting the general notification obligations under T8.1, the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus and Users agree to review at regular intervals which Operations and which Incidents must be notified.

---

### T8.1.5 NOTIFICATION OF OPERATION

**T8.1.5.1** The Transmission System Operator of Cyprus is required to notify the User of Operations on the Transmission System that, in its reasonable judgment, have (or may have) an Operational Impact on that User. Unless otherwise agreed with the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus, the User is not permitted to transmit information provided to them via a notification from the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus, pursuant to T8.1, to third parties.

**T8.1.5.2** In cases where the prescribed procedures cannot be followed before an Operation occurs, or if the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus undertakes emergency Operations without prior notification to the User, then, unless it concerns a temporary incident (lasting less than 15 minutes) that has already been resolved, the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus is required to inform the User immediately regarding these Operations. The Transmission System Operator of Cyprus is also required to inform the User of the probable duration of the relevant situation and keep them informed of updated forecasts. The Transmission System Operator of Cyprus is additionally required to inform the User immediately upon the time of termination of the incident.

**T8.1.5.3** The User is required to inform the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus of Operations on the User System that have (or may have) an Operational Impact on the Transmission System. The Transmission System Operator of Cyprus is entitled to use this information to notify other Users on whose Systems, according to the reasonable judgment of the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus, the operation has (or may have) an Operational Impact pursuant to T8.1.

<!-- PAGE 169 -->

**Version 1.1.0**
**T8 – Information Exchange**  
**Page 169**

---

**T8.1.5.4** Any exchange of information between TSOC and User, which is conducted in Writing, may be stored by TSOC, in accordance with the provisions of the Electricity Market Rules.

### T8.1.6 TYPE OF OPERATION NOTIFICATION

**T8.1.6.1** A notification (and any response to inquiries pursuant to T8.1.6.3) of an Operation clearly describes the Operation (without necessarily stating the cause) so that the recipient can assess the consequences and risks it creates.

**T8.1.6.2** The notification includes the name (and official capacity) of the natural person making the notification of the Operation on behalf of the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus or the User, as the case may be.

**T8.1.6.3** The recipient of the notification is entitled to request additional clarifications, and the sender is obligated to provide them as fully as possible.

### T8.1.7 RECORDING OF AN OPERATION

The notification shall be in Writing, whenever possible, before the Operation takes place. In urgent cases where Written notification prior to the Operation is impossible, the notification shall be made by another method and subsequently submitted in Writing as well.

### T8.1.8 TIMEFRAME WITH RESPECT TO AN OPERATION

A notification pursuant to T8.1.5 must reach its recipient prior to the performance of the operation and within a timeframe that allows for the study and assessment of the risks that may be caused by it.

### T8.1.9 INCIDENT NOTIFICATION

**T8.1.9.1** The Transmission System Operator of Cyprus notifies the User of Incidents that, in its reasonable judgment, are Significant System Incidents and have an Operational Impact on the User. Unless otherwise agreed with the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus, the User is not permitted to disseminate the information provided to them via notifications from the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus to other persons.

**T8.1.9.2** The User notifies the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus of Incidents that may be Significant System Incidents and may have impacts on the Transmission System. The Transmission System Operator of Cyprus is permitted to make use of this information when notifying the Significant System Incident to other Users, on whose Systems an Operational Impact exists or may exist, in the reasonable judgment of the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus. Any emergency Unavailability Declarations submitted by Users are also subject to this regime.

### T8.1.10 TYPE OF INCIDENT NOTIFICATION

<!-- PAGE 170 -->

**Version 1.1.0** | **T8 – Information Exchange** | **Page 170**

---

### T8.1.10.1
A notification (and any response to queries pursuant to T8.1.10.3) of an Incident shall clearly describe the Incident in accordance with T8.1.9, although it is not mandatory to state the cause of the Incident, so that the recipient can assess the consequences and risks arising from it.

### T8.1.10.2
The notification shall include the name (and official capacity) of the natural person making the Incident notification on behalf of the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) or the User, as applicable.

### T8.1.10.3
The recipient of the notification is entitled to request additional clarifications, and the sender is obligated (without being required to mandatorily state the cause of the Incident) to provide them as comprehensively as possible.

---

### T8.1.11 PROVISION OF ADDITIONAL INFORMATION

The Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) is obligated to provide to the Generator who notified an Incident pursuant to T8.1, any information requested by the Generator, so as to enable an accurate assessment of the consequences of the specific event on their facilities.

---

### T8.1.12 RECORDING OF AN INCIDENT

The notification of an Incident pursuant to T8.1.9 shall be verbal at the time of the Incident. Significant System Incidents shall be notified in Writing if requested by the User or the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC).

---

### T8.1.13 JOINT INVESTIGATION

#### T8.1.13.1
When a Significant System Incident (or a series of Significant System Incidents) is declared and a relevant report (or reports) has been prepared pursuant to T8, the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) and the User who served or received the Written report pursuant to T8, shall be entitled to request a joint investigation of the Significant System Incident.

#### T8.1.13.2
When a series of Significant System Incidents occurs (that is, when a Significant System Incident causes or exacerbates another Significant System Incident), the party requesting the joint investigation or the recipient of such a request shall be entitled to request that the joint investigation also include the other Significant System Incident (or Significant System Incidents).

#### T8.1.13.3
The Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) or a User shall be entitled to request another User to also participate in the joint investigation. The obligation of Users to participate shall be decided by the TSOC.

#### T8.1.13.4
A joint investigation shall take place only upon agreement between the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) and the involved User or Users (the agreement shall also include the determination of the parties that...

<!-- PAGE 171 -->

**Version 1.1.0** | **T8 – Information Exchange** | **Page 171**

***

...will participate in accordance with T8.1.13.3). The method and rules of the procedure and all related matters (including, as applicable, terms regarding costs and the case where an involved party withdraws from the joint investigation after the procedure has commenced) for conducting the joint investigation shall be agreed at the time the relevant procedure commences, and, in the event of non-agreement, the joint investigation shall not take place.

**T8.1.13.5** Requests for conducting a joint investigation shall be submitted in Writing.

### T8.1.14 TIME LIMIT IN RELATION TO AN INCIDENT

A notification of an Incident pursuant to T8.1.9 shall be given immediately following the Incident or from the moment the Incident becomes known or is expected by the person issuing the notification pursuant to T8.1. In any case, the notification must be given within fifteen minutes from the moment the Incident became known, unless an emergency condition exists.

---

## T8.2 OPERATIONAL COMMUNICATION AND DATA RECORDING

### T8.2.1 INTRODUCTION

**T8.2.1.1** It is necessary to establish adequate telecommunication facilities and procedures between the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) and Users to enable the timely transfer of information, so that the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) can operate the Transmission System in accordance with its undertaken obligations.

### T8.2.2 PURPOSE

**T8.2.2.1** The purpose of T8.2 is:
* (a) To establish contact points for the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) and each category of Users.
* (b) To describe in detail the telecommunication equipment required between the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) and each category of Users.
* (c) To establish general procedures relating to the following matters that do not contravene any specific procedures that may be specified in other parts of the Transmission Rules:
  * (i) General information exchange between the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) and Users.
  * (ii) Authorization of the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) personnel representing it and the User personnel representing them in the exchange of information between them.
  * (iii) Recording of data.

**T8.2.2.2** Pursuant to T8.2, the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) and Users shall adopt the use of new technologies and methods for information exchange whenever it is demonstrated that a reasonable benefit exists and their implementation is reasonable under the prevailing conditions.

<!-- PAGE 172 -->

**Document Header:**
*Version 1.1.0 | T8 – Information Exchange*  
*Page 172*

---

### T8.2.2.3
T8.2 covers general procedures for all forms of communication of operational information between the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) and Users, excluding communication regarding actions for their connection to the Power System, which is specified in the Transmission System Connection Conditions. Data concerning energy measurements for Electricity Market purposes are not specifically covered by T8.2.

### T8.2.2.4
No provision of T8.2 repeals or replaces the provisions of the Electricity Market Rules or those set out elsewhere in the Transmission Rules regarding the exchange of information and data between Users and the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC), as well as regarding the use of the Market Management System for such exchange.

---

### T8.2.3 SCOPE OF APPLICATION

#### T8.2.3.1
T8.2 applies to the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) and to Users, who in T8 are the following:
* **(a)** Generators, for Generating Units connected to the Transmission System, Generating Units connected to the Distribution System for which a Connection Agreement has been concluded with TSOC, as well as for other Balancing Service Providers they represent;
* **(b)** Distribution System Operator;
* **(c)** Consumers connected to the Transmission System;
* **(d)** Independent Aggregators;
* **(e)** Transmission System Owner;
* **(f)** Distribution System Owner;
* **(g)** Operators of Energy Storage Facilities connected to the Transmission System.

---

### T8.2.4 CONTACT POINTS

#### T8.2.4.1 TRANSMISSION SYSTEM OPERATOR OF CYPRUS

##### T8.2.4.1.1
Unless specifically defined otherwise in other parts of the Transmission and Distribution Rules, the contact point of the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) for operational matters concerning the Transmission System in real time shall be the National Energy Control Centre (NECC).

##### T8.2.4.1.2
The Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) shall, at regular intervals, inform Users of the contact points of the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) (and their relevant characteristics) and of any changes to them and/or their relevant characteristics for the purposes of each part of the Transmission Rules (including, as appropriate, the specific characteristics for each part). The User shall communicate with the specific contact points that have been notified.

##### T8.2.4.1.3
The Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) shall, at regular intervals, distribute to Users an organizational chart and list of authorized personnel and contact numbers (in accordance with... [text continues on next page])

<!-- PAGE 173 -->

**Version 1.1.0** | **T8 – Information Exchange** | **Page 173**

---

...the notification given in T8.2.4.1.2), in order to facilitate the User in communicating with the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC).

### T8.2.4.2 GENERATORS

**T8.2.4.2.1** The Generator shall notify the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) of the contact points and authorized personnel (referred to in T8.2.4.2) of the Generator prior to their connection to the Power System and, subsequently, update this information whenever required.

**T8.2.4.2.2** The Generator shall maintain Control Facilities. The Generator, acting in accordance with Good Industry Practice, shall staff the Control Facilities for all time intervals during which it participates in the Balancing Market or provides Replacement Reserve (RR), with personnel possessing appropriate formal qualifications. Participation in the Balancing Market is defined as the submission of bids for upward or downward balancing energy and the provision of reserves.

**T8.2.4.2.3** The Control Facilities shall be staffed by one or more Safety Coordinators who are responsible for maintaining Safety from the System in accordance with the terms of T12. Furthermore, it shall be staffed by one or more Authorized Operators, who may concurrently serve as Safety Coordinators, responsible for communicating with the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) without undue delay. The Authorized Operators shall possess appropriate experience and training and shall be authorized to perform the following tasks on behalf of the Generator:

* **(a)** to receive and execute Dispatch Instructions;
* **(b)** to receive and confirm receipt of requests regarding various matters, such as operation outside the limits of the Registered Operational Characteristics, Availability, capability to provide Ancillary Services (where applicable), and the Operational Characteristics of Generating Units during Emergency Conditions.

**T8.2.4.2.4** The Generator shall at all times designate an individual as an Authorized Representative and notify the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) accordingly. The Authorized Representative is responsible for liaising with the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) on all matters concerning the Transmission Rules, except for those specified in T8.2.4.2.2 and T8.2.4.2.3. In the event that the Authorized Representative is not located at the Control Facilities, the Authorized Representative must be able to maintain continuous contact with the Control Facilities. If the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) requires the Authorized Representative to contact the National Energy Control Center, the Authorized Representative must respond to this request without delay and no later than 15 minutes from the time of the request.

**T8.2.4.2.5** The Authorized Representative shall be authorized by the Generator to perform the following tasks on behalf of the Generator:

* **(a)** To make assessments, acting in accordance with Good Industry Practice, regarding the current Availability,

<!-- PAGE 174 -->

**Document:** Issue 1.1.0 | T8 – Exchange of Information  
**Page:** 174  

---

[...continued from previous page]

...the capability to provide Ancillary Services (where applicable), and the Operating Characteristics of the Generation Units.  
(b) To make Declarations of Change of Operating Characteristics for each Generation Unit.  
(c) To communicate on matters concerning Reservations / Withholdings of Generation Units.  

The Producer may, at regular intervals and following appropriate notification to the TSOC, revise the contact points and the relevant authorized personnel.  

**T8.2.4.2.6** It is clarified that the provisions in this Section T8.2.4.2 shall not apply in cases where the corresponding Producer is represented by an Independent Aggregator.  

### T8.2.4.3 INDEPENDENT AGGREGATORS  

**T8.2.4.3.1** The provisions set forth in Section T8.2.4.3 apply to Independent Aggregators regarding the portfolios they represent, provided that they participate in the Balancing Market or provide Replacement Reserves (RR). Participation in the Balancing Market is defined as the submission of bids for upward or downward balancing energy and the provision of reserves.  

**T8.2.4.3.2** The Independent Aggregator shall notify the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) of its contact points and authorized personnel upon its accession as a Contracting Party to the contractual framework of the Electricity Market Rules, in accordance with the procedure specified in the Electricity Market Rules, and shall update this information whenever required.  

**T8.2.4.3.3** The Independent Aggregator shall maintain Control Facilities, which may also enable remote operation. The Independent Aggregator, acting in accordance with Good Utility Practice, shall staff the Control Facilities with personnel possessing appropriate formal qualifications at all times during which it participates in the Balancing Market or provides Replacement Reserves (RR), if so requested by the TSOC.  

**T8.2.4.3.4** The Control Facilities shall be staffed by one or more Authorized Operators, who shall be responsible for communicating with the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) without undue delay. Authorized Operators shall possess suitable experience and training and shall be authorized to perform the following tasks on behalf of the Independent Aggregator:  
(a) to receive and execute Dispatch Instructions;  
(b) to receive and confirm receipt of requests regarding various operational matters, such as operation outside the limits of Registered Operating Characteristics, the capability to provide Ancillary Services (where applicable), and the Operating Characteristics of the portfolios represented by the Independent Aggregator during Emergency Conditions.

<!-- PAGE 175 -->

Version 1.1.0 | T8 – Information Exchange  
Page 175

***

### T8.2.4.3.5
The Independent Aggregator shall always designate an individual as an Authorized Representative and notify the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC). The Authorized Representative shall be responsible for liaising with the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) on all matters relating to the Transmission Rules, except for those specified in T8.2.4.3.3 and T8.2.4.3.4. In cases where the Authorized Representative is not present at the Control Facilities, the Authorized Representative must be able to remain in continuous contact with the Control Facilities. Should the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) require from the Control Facilities that the Authorized Representative contact the National Energy Control Center (NECC), the Authorized Representative shall respond to this request without delay and at the latest within 15 minutes from the time of the request.

### T8.2.4.3.6
The Authorized Representative shall be authorized by the Independent Aggregator to perform the following duties on its behalf regarding:
* Each RES or High-Efficiency CHP Generation Unit it represents;
* The portfolio of RES or High-Efficiency CHP Generation Units it represents, provided it constitutes an Ancillary Services Provider (ASP);
* Each Demand Load AZ it represents;
* Each Demand Load AZ portfolio it represents;
* Each Electricity Storage Facility it represents;
* Each Electricity Storage Facility portfolio it represents:

(a) Make estimations, acting in accordance with Good Utility Practice, regarding the capability to provide Ancillary Services and the Operational Characteristics of each ASP represented in the Electricity Market by the Independent Aggregator.  
(b) Issue Declarations of Change in Operational Characteristics individually for each RES or High-Efficiency CHP Generation Unit, Demand Load AZ, or Electricity Storage Facility, as well as cumulatively for each portfolio it represents.

The Independent Aggregator may periodically, and upon relevant notification to TSOC, revise its communication contact points and the relevant authorized personnel.

---

### T8.2.4.4 DISTRIBUTION SYSTEM OPERATOR

#### T8.2.4.4.1
The Distribution System Operator shall notify the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) of its communication contact points and its authorized personnel, and update this information whenever required.

#### T8.2.4.4.2
The Distribution System Operator shall maintain Control Facilities at all its Regional (or local) Offices. The Distribution System Operator, acting in accordance with Good Utility Practice, shall staff its Control Facilities during working hours, and all Distribution System Control Operators shall be available to the Transmission System Operator...

<!-- PAGE 176 -->

**Edition 1.1.0** | **T8 – Information Exchange**  
**Page 176**

...Cyprus at all times. In the event that the Distribution System Operator establishes Regional Control Centers with direct 24-hour monitoring responsibilities, these shall be staffed at all times.

**T8.2.4.4.3** The Distribution System Operator shall operate its Switching Facilities in accordance with the terms contained in the relevant agreements with the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC). The execution of such agreements shall not be unreasonably delayed.

### T8.2.4.5 TRANSMISSION SYSTEM OWNER

**T8.2.4.5.1** The Transmission System Owner shall notify the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) of its contact points and authorized personnel, and shall update this information whenever required.

### T8.2.4.6 TRANSMISSION-CONNECTED CONSUMERS

**T8.2.4.6.1** A Consumer connected to the Transmission System shall notify the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) of its contact points and authorized personnel (referred to in T8.2.4.3) prior to its connection to the Transmission System and, subsequently, shall update this information whenever required.

**T8.2.4.6.2** A Consumer connected to the Transmission System shall provide the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) with all information regarding one or more Authorized Operators, with whom the TSOC can communicate directly and without delay (except in cases where it has been agreed otherwise between the TSOC and the Transmission-connected Consumer), and who possess the required formal qualifications and relevant experience and are authorized to perform operations on behalf of the Transmission-connected Consumer.

**T8.2.4.6.3** The Authorized Operator must be capable of being present at the Facility Premises of the Transmission-connected Consumer within a time period of 60 minutes, if requested by the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC).

**T8.2.4.6.4** A Consumer connected to the Transmission System shall designate an individual as an Authorized Representative and inform the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) accordingly. The Authorized Representative is responsible for liaising with the TSOC on all matters concerning the Transmission Rules, except for those specified in T8.2.4.6.2 and T8.2.4.6.3. In the event that the Authorized Representative is not present at the Facility Premises of the Transmission-connected Consumer, the Authorized Representative must be continuously in contact with the Facility Premises of the Transmission-connected Consumer. In the event that the TSOC requires from the Facility Premises of the Transmission-connected Consumer that its Authorized Representative contact the National Energy Control Center (NECC), the Authorized Representative shall comply with this requirement...

<!-- PAGE 177 -->

**Edition 1.1.0 | Module T8 – Information Exchange**  
**Page 177**

---

...this without delay and no later than 15 minutes from the time of demand.

**T8.2.4.6.5** The Authorized Representative shall be authorized by the Consumer connected to the Transmission System to provide the Transmission System Operator, whenever requested, on a periodic or ad-hoc basis, acting in accordance with Good Industry Practice, estimates regarding the Maximum Expected Power Demand for each Meter of the Consumer connected to the Transmission System. The provided estimate constitutes the Declared Maximum Expected Power Demand for the corresponding Meter.

**T8.2.4.6.6** It is clarified that the provisions of this T8.2.4.6 do not apply in case the relevant Consumer is represented by an Independent Aggregator.

### T8.2.4.7 ELECTRICITY STORAGE FACILITY OPERATORS

**T8.2.4.7.1** The Storage Facility Operator shall notify the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) of its contact points and authorized personnel prior to connection to the Power System and shall subsequently update this information whenever required.

**T8.2.4.7.2** The Storage Facility Operator shall maintain Control Facilities capable of enabling remote operation. The Storage Facility Operator, acting in accordance with Good Industry Practice, shall staff the Control Facilities for all time intervals during which it participates in the Balancing Market or provides Replacement Reserve (RR), with appropriately qualified personnel, if so required by the TSOC.

**T8.2.4.7.3** The Control Facilities shall be staffed by one or more Safety Coordinators responsible for maintaining Safety from the System in accordance with the terms of Module T12. In addition, they shall be staffed by one or more Authorized Operators, who may concurrently act as Safety Coordinators, responsible for communicating with the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus without undue delay. The Authorized Operators shall possess appropriate experience and training and shall be authorized to perform the following tasks on behalf of the Storage Facility Operator:

*   **(a)** receive and execute Instructions;
*   **(b)** receive and confirm receipt of requests regarding various matters, such as operation outside the Registered Operating Characteristics, Availability, capability to provide Ancillary Services (where applicable), and Operating Characteristics of the Storage Facilities during Emergency Conditions.

**T8.2.4.7.4** The Storage Facility Operator shall at all times designate an individual as Authorized Representative and notify the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus accordingly. The Authorized Representative shall be responsible for liaising with the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus on all matters pertaining to the Transmission Rules, except for those specified in T8.2.4.7.2 and T8.2.4.7.3. In the event that the...

<!-- PAGE 178 -->

**Version 1.1.0** | **T8 – Information Exchange** | **Page 178**

---

...Authorized Representative is not located at the Operational Facilities, the Authorized Representative must be able to be in contact with the Operational Facilities continuously. In the event that the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) requires the Authorized Representative to contact the National Energy Control Center, the Authorized Representative shall respond to this requirement without delay and no later than 15 minutes from the time of the request.

**T8.2.4.7.5** The Authorized Representative shall be authorized by the Storage Facility Operator to perform the following tasks on behalf of the Storage Facility Operator:
(a) Make assessments, acting in accordance with Good Industry Practice rules regarding the existing Availability, the capability to provide Ancillary Services (where applicable), and the Operational Characteristics of the Storage Facility.
(b) Make Declarations of Change of Operational Characteristics for the Storage Facility.
(c) Communicate on matters concerning Reservations of the Storage Facility.

The Storage Facility Operator may, at regular intervals and following relative notification to the TSOC, revise the contact points and the relevant authorized personnel.

**T8.2.4.7.6** It is clarified that the provisions of this T8.2.4.7 do not apply in cases where the corresponding Storage Facility is represented by an Independent Aggregator.

### T8.2.5 TELECOMMUNICATION EQUIPMENT

**T8.2.5.1** The absolutely necessary telecommunication equipment to be installed and maintained for communication between the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) and the User is specified in T8.2.5. The relevant obligation to provide signals arises in accordance with the provisions of T1.12 and T7.5.

**T8.2.5.2** All equipment procured by Users in accordance with T8.2.5 must comply with the applicable standards of the International Telecommunication Union (ITU) and the International Electrotechnical Commission (IEC) for SCADA/EMS systems and telecommunications equipment. Furthermore, it must comply with the standards notified by the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC), exercising reasonable judgment, prior to design or procurement (whichever occurs later). The procurement cost of this equipment shall be borne by the User, unless otherwise specified.

**T8.2.5.3** The Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) reserves the right to modify the requirements referred to in T8.2.5.2 if deemed necessary for the smooth operation of the Transmission System. Users are obliged to bear the cost of installing any additional required equipment, within the time frame specified by the TSOC. The TSOC may partially or fully exempt specific Users from this requirement, if deemed necessary.

**T8.2.5.4** SUPERVISORY CONTROL AND DATA ACQUISITION AND ENERGY MANAGEMENT SYSTEM (SCADA/EMS)

<!-- PAGE 179 -->

| Version 1.1.0 | T8 – Information Exchange | Page 179 |
|---|---|---|

**T8.2.5.4.1** Users must possess appropriate equipment for providing signals and indications to the TSOC in accordance with the provisions of T8.2.5.4.2.

**T8.2.5.4.2** SCADA/EMS terminal equipment or other connection equipment is required for the transmission of signals and indications from the User Installation Site to the National Control Center and vice versa. The signals and indications that must be made available by the User for transmission via the SCADA/EMS equipment to the National Control Center are the signals and indications referred to in T1 and T7, together with any information that the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus may reasonably request from Users from time to time. The communication protocols that the User may utilize must first be approved by the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus.

**T8.2.5.4.3** Depending on the technology available to the User, there are different interface capabilities for the signals and indications of the User Installation Site.

**T8.2.5.4.3.1** In the event that the User utilizes a Remote Terminal Unit (RTU), the following must be taken into account (as applicable, where relevant):

* **(a)** Terminal equipment will be required in the control room of the Transmission Substation required at the User Installation Site for the transmission of signals and indications to and from the National Control Center.
* **(b)** Connection and interface cabinets must be installed in the control room of the Transmission Substation located at the User Installation Site. The procurement and maintenance of cabling and signaling from the User's Facilities and Machinery to the User's connection and interface cabinets shall be the responsibility of the User or the Transmission System Owner. The Transmission System Operator of Cyprus shall provide full specifications that will ensure the technical characteristics and quality of telecommunication cables for interconnecting these connection and interface cabinets. For Users who do not have prior installations with this type of telecommunication connection, a procurement and installation procedure shall be followed with the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus, which will ensure the required minimum technical characteristics and quality.

**T8.2.5.4.3.2** In the event that the User utilizes distributed control technology in which signals and indications are transmitted via a local communications network, provision must necessarily be made for the following (as applicable, where relevant):

* **(a)** Interface equipment for signals and indications from the User Installation Site must be provided by the User in accordance with the Connection Agreement and may be modified at regular intervals, depending on the development of the communications system and the technology available to the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus at that time.
* **(b)** The procurement and maintenance of cabling and signaling from the User's Facilities and Machinery to the User's interface equipment shall be the responsibility of the User. The Transmission System Operator of Cyprus provides full

<!-- PAGE 180 -->

**Version 1.1.0** | **T8 – Information Exchange** | **Page 180**

---

...specifications that will ensure the technical characteristics and quality of the connection medium (e.g., serial connection) with the User's interface equipment (User gateway) at the Transmission Substation located at the User's Facility Site or the Transmission System Owner's site.

### T8.2.5.5 COMPUTER EQUIPMENT

**T8.2.5.5.1** Each User must comply with the specifications of the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) and procure suitable computer and data network equipment allowing data exchange, such as e-mail, Operating Instructions, etc., between the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) and the User. This equipment shall be used by the User exclusively for operational communication with the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC). The use of these computers for other applications is not permitted.

**T8.2.5.5.2** Each User shall be responsible for optimizing the operational reliability and security of the computer equipment referred to in T8.2.5.5.1, while making provision for an uninterrupted power supply (UPS). Attention is also drawn to the specific provisions regarding the Market Management System in Chapter 1, paragraph 3.2 of the Electricity Market Rules.

### T8.2.5.6 TELEPHONES / FACSIMILE DEVICES

**T8.2.5.6.1** Each User must procure and maintain (at their own expense) telephone and facsimile (fax) devices as required under T8.2.5.6. The provisions of the relevant paragraph of the Electricity Market Rules regarding the provision of information from a Contracting Party to the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) are also applicable.

**T8.2.5.6.2** Users must possess at least two separate circuits for the Public Switched Telephone Network (PSTN) at their Control Facilities, which shall operate continuously.

**T8.2.5.6.3** Users must possess at least one facsimile device at their Control Facilities connected to a dedicated circuit of the Public Switched Telephone Network (PSTN), which shall operate continuously.

### T8.2.5.7 ACCESS AND SECURITY

**T8.2.5.7.1** SCADA/EMS equipment, metering equipment, computer equipment, and telecommunication equipment connected to the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) must be secured against unauthorized access. Security and access procedures shall be agreed with Users in accordance with the Operating Instructions, but must allow the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) access to this equipment and associated information for fault investigation, testing, and data collection purposes, either jointly with a representative of the User or the Transmission System Owner, or exclusively by personnel of the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC), provided that the User or the Transmission System Owner is simultaneously informed.

<!-- PAGE 181 -->

**Version 1.1.0** | **T8 – Information Exchange** | **Page 181**

---

### T8.2.5.8 TIME STANDARDS

**T8.2.5.8.1** Time is regulated in accordance with the standard time specified by the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC). The reference time is transmitted to the corresponding telecommunication devices, so that time is measured in a uniform manner. Alternatively, Users may install proprietary equipment for GPS (Global Positioning System) and synchronize all their devices to it.

### T8.2.6 COMMUNICATION BETWEEN TRANSMISSION SYSTEM OPERATOR AND USER

**T2.6.1** In the event that the National Energy Control Centre moves to another location, the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) shall notify Users immediately upon taking the decision to move and at a time period greater than or equal to seven (7) days prior to the relocation. In case of urgent need, it may notify Users immediately after the relocation as quickly as practicable.

**T8.2.6.2** Unless otherwise specified in other parts of the Transmission Code, all Instructions of the National Energy Control Centre and communication between the National Energy Control Centre and the User's Switching/Operational Control Facilities shall be conducted using the means described in T8.2.5.

**T8.2.6.3** Automatic recordings of communications (by any means) made using telephones, electronic media, facsimile transmission, or telex, shall be accepted by the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) and Users as proof of such Instructions or communications.

### T8.2.7 DATA AND NOTICES

**T8.2.7.1** The data and notices submitted to the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) and to Users in accordance with the Transmission Code (other than data and notices whose transmission method is specified by special provisions of the Transmission Code) shall be in Writing and shall be delivered in person or sent by prepaid letter, telex, via the Market Management System platform, by electronic mail with proof of delivery option, or by facsimile.

**T8.2.7.2** The data and notices submitted to the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) in accordance with the Transmission Code must be addressed to the person and address that the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) has notified to Users for this purpose in the Connection Agreement, or to such other person or address as the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) may notify to Users.

**T8.2.7.3** The data and notices submitted to Users in accordance with the Transmission Code must be addressed to the person authorized by the User (at the address designated by Users to the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) for this purpose in the Connection Agreement and, if this is not possible, at the recipient's head office) or to such other person or address as Users may from time to time re-designate to the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC).

<!-- PAGE 182 -->

**Version 1.1.0** | **T8 – Information Exchange** | **Page 182**

---

**T8.2.7.4** All data, as applicable, shall be provided with reference to nominal Voltage and Frequency, unless specified otherwise.

**T8.2.7.5** All Operational Data shall be provided in accordance with the schedule specified by the Transmission Rules and the Electricity Market Rules.

### T8.2.8 DATA REGISTRATION

**T8.2.8.1** Operational Data are considered all data required to be provided by TSOC or the Users in accordance with the Transmission Rules and the Electricity Market Rules, as well as all data designated as Operational Data pursuant to the Transmission Rules. Operational Data provided by the User shall be sent to the department or address designated by TSOC from time to time.

**T8.2.8.2** TSOC and the Users shall treat all Operational Data as confidential.

**T8.2.8.3** TSOC shall maintain a complete and accurate record of all Operational Data submitted to or stored by it in accordance with the Transmission Rules and the Electricity Market Rules. TSOC shall select the format of the data storage archives (provided that their format does not impair access to and understanding of them by the User in accordance with T8.2.8.4). All Operational Data shall be retained for at least six (6) years from the date on which the Operational Data were first provided (or created, whichever is earlier).

**T8.2.8.4** TSOC shall allow Users access to the records (and the creation of copies) of Operational Data and/or data required to be stored in accordance with T8.2.8.3, upon reasonable notice, and in accordance with the provisions of the Electricity Market Rules.

---

### T8.3 INFORMATION EXCHANGE WITHIN THE ELECTRICITY MARKET FRAMEWORK

#### T8.3.1 INTRODUCTION

**T8.3.1.1** To enable TSOC to fulfill its obligations within the framework of the Electricity Market, it requires immediate notification of any change in the Registered Operational Characteristics of Dispatchable Generating Units, Demand Facilities connected to the Transmission System, and other Market Participants.

**T8.3.1.2** TSOC prepares an auxiliary Short-Term Demand Forecast. It provides Market Participants with a Load Demand Forecast and a RES Injection Forecast, prepared in accordance with T3, for each Trading Day.

<!-- PAGE 183 -->

**Version 1.1.0** | **T8 – Information Exchange** | **Page 183**

---

**T8.3.1.3** It is emphasized that matters regarding the provision of information concerning Market Participants are also regulated by the provisions of the Electricity Market Rules relating to the Day-Ahead Market and the Balancing Market, as well as the provisions of the Electricity Market Rules regarding the interface between these markets. The arrangements under T8.3 function as complementary to these provisions of the Electricity Market Rules.

### T8.3.2 PURPOSE

**T8.3.2.1** The purpose of T8.3 is the completion of the information exchange framework between the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) and Users, in order for the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) to respond more effectively to its obligations within the framework of the Electricity Market.

**T8.3.2.2** To achieve the primary objective, procedures and communication means for the exchange of specific information are described in T8.3.4.

### T8.3.3 SCOPE OF APPLICATION

**T8.3.3.1** The provisions of T8.3 apply to the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) and all Users, who in this T8.3 are defined as:
* **(a)** Producers, for Generation Units connected to the Transmission System, Generation Units connected to the Distribution System for which a Connection Agreement has been concluded with TSOC, as well as for other Generation Units that they represent within the framework of the Electricity Market;
* **(b)** Storage Facility Operators;
* **(c)** Consumers connected to the Transmission System;
* **(d)** Independent Aggregators.

### T8.3.4 DEMAND AND RES INJECTION FORECAST

**T8.3.4.1** The Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) shall provide daily to Market Participants a Demand Forecast and a RES Injection Forecast for each Trading Period for the next Trading Day, which shall also include Transmission System and Distribution System losses. This information shall be provided in a timely manner via publication on the website of the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC), in the format specified by the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC).

### T8.3.5 DECLARATIONS OF CHANGE IN OPERATIONAL CHARACTERISTICS

**T8.3.5.1** Users are obligated to submit to the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) a Declaration of Change in Operational Characteristics for each Generation / Demand Entity they represent, immediately in any case where their Registered Operational Characteristics are altered.

**T8.3.5.2** The submission of a Declaration of Change in Operational Characteristics by a User for each Generation / Demand Entity it represents must be carried out in accordance with the terms of Article T8.3.6.

<!-- PAGE 184 -->

**Version 1.1.0** | **T8 – Information Exchange** | **Page 184**

---

**T8.3.5.3** Immediately following the receipt of the Declarations of Change of Operating Characteristics from the Users, the Electricity Market Operator shall be informed.

**T8.3.5.4** Each User shall immediately inform the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) when the values of the Registered Operating Characteristics of the Generation / Demand Entities it represents are expected to change within the next 7 days.

---

### T8.3.6 MEANS OF COMMUNICATION FOR DECLARATIONS OF CHANGE OF OPERATING CHARACTERISTICS

**T8.3.6.1** Users shall submit Declarations of Change of Operating Characteristics using an appropriate electronic communication medium that is compatible with the reasonable requirements of the information system of the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC), which must be installed at each Generation Site or at other suitable Operational Control Facilities.

**T8.3.6.2** To ensure the rapid transfer of information during the time interval between the Day-Ahead Market Gate Closure and real-time operation, Users must submit Declarations of Change of Operating Characteristics verbally (via telephone) in the first instance, and subsequently confirm them (without delay) In Writing in the manner described in T8.3.6.1. In all other cases except those described above, Users shall submit Declarations of Change of Operating Characteristics in the manner described in T8.3.5.

**T8.3.6.3** In the event that the electronic communication medium has suffered a failure or is generally unavailable for any reason, the submission of Declarations of Change of Operating Characteristics shall be performed using any other approved communication medium, in accordance with the provisions described in T8.

\<End of T8\>

## T9 - OPERATIONAL CHECKS
