---

# T12 - SAFETY RULES

## T12.1 INTRODUCTION

**T12.1.1**  
The "Safety Rules" (T12) specify the standard procedures to be implemented by the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) and Users for the coordination, implementation, and maintenance of necessary Safety Precautions when work is to be carried out on the Transmission System or on a User System, and when Safety Precautions are required on High Voltage Equipment so that such work can be safely executed. The term "work" includes all Tests, except for Tests under abnormal conditions.

**T12.1.2**  
T12 does not apply in cases where Safety Precautions are to be agreed exclusively between Users.

**T12.1.3**  
T12 covers the procedures required to be carried out so that a High Voltage Equipment item is taken out of service, adequately Isolated and Earthed, such that it cannot become live and work on it can be safely executed.

### T12.1.4 Definition of Terms

**T12.1.4.1**  
Users must be aware that exclusively within T12, the terminology used is intended to be identical to that used by the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) and specific User Safety Regulations. For this reason, the term High Voltage Equipment is restricted and used accordingly in T12. Therefore, Users must be careful regarding this term when reading and applying T12.

**T12.1.4.2**  
In T12 only, the following terms have the corresponding meanings:

**T12.1.4.2.1**  
**"High Voltage Equipment"** means an apparatus or set of apparatus forming part of the High Voltage electrical circuit(s) of the User System or the Transmission System, on which System Safety may be required or on which Safety Precautions must be applied, so that work can be executed on it.

**T12.1.4.2.2**  
**"Isolation"** means the disconnection of the Equipment from the remainder of the User System or Transmission System in which such Equipment is installed, by one of the following methods:

(a) An Isolating Device is placed in an isolating position. For the isolating position, one of the following must apply:

(i) Maintained fixed by Locking the Isolating Device in the isolating position and affixing a Caution Notice. When the Isolating Device is Locked with a Safety Key, the Safety Key must be kept in a Key Box which, for safety purposes, features two separate locks. One Key of the Key Box shall be held by the responsible...

<!-- PAGE 215 -->

...the earthing of the Apparatus and the other in the possession of the person responsible for carrying out the work on the Apparatus.

(ii) Be maintained and/or secured by another method that complies with the Local Safety Rules of the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) or the corresponding User, as applicable.

(b) There is adequate physical isolation which must comply with and be maintained in accordance with a method specified in the Local Safety Rules of the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) or the corresponding User, as applicable, and, if included in the procedures of that method, a Warning Notice must be placed at the point of isolation.

***

### T12.1.4.2.3
**"Earthing"** means the provision of a connection between conductors and earth via an Earthing Device which may:

(a) Be fixed and Locked in the earthing position. When the Earthing Device is Locked with a Safety Key, the Safety Key must be secured in a Key Safe Box which, for safety purposes, is equipped with two separate locks. One Key of the Key Safe Box shall be in the possession of the person responsible for the earthing of the Apparatus and the other in the possession of the person responsible for carrying out the work on the Apparatus.

(b) There is adequate physical isolation which must comply with and be maintained in accordance with a method specified in the Local Safety Rules of the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) or the corresponding User, as applicable, and, if included in the procedures of that method, a Warning Notice must be placed at the point of isolation.

***

### T12.1.4.2.4 Authorized Person
Is a person who holds a valid Certificate of Authorization, issued in accordance with the provisions of the Transmission Rules, which specifies:

i) the type of work which the Authorized Person has been authorized to carry out, and  
ii) the maximum nominal Voltage of the system to which it applies.

***

### T12.1.4.3
The term **EAC** is used in T12 with reference to the capacities of the Electricity Authority of Cyprus as Transmission System Owner, Distribution System Operator, and Distribution System Owner, as applicable.

***

## T12.2 PURPOSE

### T12.2.1
The purpose of T12 is to ensure Safety from the System when work on a User System or on the Transmission System requires the implementation of Safety Precautions on High Voltage Apparatus of another User System or of the Transmission System up to a Connection Point.

***

## T12.3 SCOPE

### T12.3.1
T12 applies to the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) and to Users, which is a term that in T12 means:

***

*Version 1.1.0 | T12 – Safety Rules | Page 215*

<!-- PAGE 216 -->

**Version 1.1.0** | **T12 – Safety Rules** | **Page 216**

---

(a) Producers connected to the Transmission System.
(b) Storage Facility Operators connected to the Transmission System.
(c) Transmission System Owner.
(d) Distribution System Operator.
(e) Distribution System Owner.
(f) Consumers connected to the Transmission System.

### T12.4 PROCEDURE

#### T12.4.1 Safety Rules

**T12.4.1.1** Safety procedures concerning personnel working on or near Transmission System Facilities and Plant are governed by the Safety Rules of the EAC (or any future revision of these Rules). The applicable Safety Rules of the Electricity Authority of Cyprus constitute an integral part of the Distribution Code and their revision shall be carried out with the consent of the TSOC.

**T12.4.1.2** In the event of a conflict between the provisions of T12 and the Safety Rules of the EAC, the provisions of the Safety Rules of the EAC shall prevail.

**T12.4.1.3** In the event of a dispute regarding the correct interpretation of a rule of the Safety Rules of the EAC or any other relevant revision thereof in force at that specific time, the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) shall provide the final interpretation.

**T12.4.1.4 Acceptance of Local Safety Rules:**

(a) In accordance with the time schedule requirements of each Connection Agreement, where applicable, the TSOC, as appropriate, shall provide the User with a copy of its Local Safety Rules pertaining to the side of the Connection Point operated by the TSOC, for each Connection Site.
(b) In accordance with the time schedule requirements of the Connection Agreement, where applicable, each User shall provide the TSOC with a copy of its Local Safety Rules pertaining to its side of the Connection Point for each Connection Site.
(c) Prior to connection, where applicable, the TSOC must have approved the relevant Local Safety Rules of the User pertaining to Isolation and Earthing.

**T12.4.1.5** Either party may request that the Isolation and/or Earthing conditions of the Local Safety Rules affecting the Connection Site be made more stringent. Provided that the request is reasonable, the other party shall implement the necessary modifications as soon as possible. These changes may require Isolation and/or Earthing at a site remote from the Connection Site, depending on the configuration of the User System or the Transmission System. The approval of modifications shall not be delayed on the grounds that the approving party considers the terms of Isolation and/or Earthing to be overly stringent.

<!-- PAGE 217 -->

| Edition 1.1.0 | T12 – Safety Rules | Page 217 |
|:---|:---:|---:|

**T12.4.1.6** If, following approval, a contracting party wishes to modify the terms of its Local Safety Regulations regarding Isolation and/or Earthing, it must notify the other contracting party. In the event that the proposed measures are more stringent, the other contracting party is not required to note the changes. In the event that the proposed measures are less stringent, the other contracting party must approve the new terms.

**T12.4.1.7** In the Transmission System Connection Terms, a Site Operational Regulation shall be specified for each User Installation, established by the TSOC in consultation with the Users. The Site Operational Regulation shall specify the boundaries of responsibility regarding the safety of personnel working or conducting tests at the User Connection Site and on the circuits passing through the User Installation at any point.

### T12.4.2 Safety Coordinators

**T12.4.2.1** The TSOC and each User shall designate and continuously maintain for each Connection Point one or more individuals (Safety Coordinators), who shall be responsible for the coordination of Safety Precautions whenever work needs to be carried out that requires the implementation of Safety Precautions on High Voltage Equipment in connection with T12. A Safety Coordinator may be responsible for safety coordination of High Voltage Equipment at more than one Connection Point.

**T12.4.2.1.1** The TSOC shall implement a systematic evaluation procedure for candidate Safety Coordinators. It is the responsibility of the TSOC to maintain up to date, in accordance with international practice and technology, the safety procedures applied to the Transmission System and the Distribution System. Changes in safety procedures and operational practice must be reflected, where applicable, in the Operation of the Distribution System and User Systems. Therefore, the TSOC shall establish a procedure for notifying matters relating to authorization evaluation as well as operational and safety issues.

**T12.4.2.2** Each Safety Coordinator must be authorized as competent to perform the functions specified in T12 in order to achieve Safety from the System. Only such authorized persons shall implement the provisions of T12. Safety Coordinators and personnel involved in the implementation of Safety Precautions shall hold a Certificate of Authorization clearly stating their role in the implementation of Safety Precautions.

High Voltage Certificates of Authorization for exercising Safety Coordinator duties at each Connection Point of the Transmission System shall be issued to qualified representatives of Users by the TSOC, in accordance with the relevant TSOC Operating Instruction published on the TSOC website, which shall specify application procedures and required documentation.

<!-- PAGE 218 -->

**Version 1.1.0** | **T12 – Safety Rules** | **Page 218**

In the event that an Operation and Maintenance Agreement is concluded between EAC and a User, for the period of validity of this agreement, the authorized persons of the EAC Area of Responsibility adjacent to the User's Area of Responsibility may perform duties of Control Engineer and Safety Coordinator in the User's Area of Responsibility, provided that the following apply:

(a) The EAC Area of Responsibility adjacent to the User's Area of Responsibility is extended so as to also cover the User's Area of Responsibility, in accordance with the provisions of the Operation and Maintenance Agreement. The extension of the Area of Responsibility shall be implemented following approval by TSOC.

(b) The Authorized Persons of EAC who will perform duties of Control Engineer and Safety Coordinator in the User's Area of Responsibility must hold the appropriate certificates in accordance with the provisions of the Transmission Rules and the Distribution Rules.

TSOC shall maintain a record of the Authorization Certificates it issues together with the relevant recommendations and the Certificates/Confirmations required in the relevant Operating Instruction. It is understood that TSOC shall conduct an examination of candidate Safety Coordinators on subjects that, inter alia, shall include safety management and coordination, legislation, etc., in order to issue a High Voltage Authorization Certificate.

**T12.4.2.3** In accordance with T1.17.2, prior to the Works Completion Date, the User must submit to the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus a written declaration that the Safety Coordinators acting on behalf of the User are authorized and competent in accordance with the provisions of T12, including a list of their names. Any change to the Safety Coordinators acting on behalf of the User must be submitted immediately and in writing to TSOC. The Transmission System Operator of Cyprus shall maintain an updated record of all Safety Coordinators authorized by TSOC and the Users, as applicable, as competent to carry out the operations referred to in T12.

**T12.4.2.4** Communications between Safety Coordinators shall be conducted via common communication channels, and separate telephone lines are not required for Safety Coordinators. At the moment of communication, each participating party shall declare that they are authorized to act as a Safety Coordinator, in accordance with T12.

**T12.4.2.5** If works are to be carried out on the User System or the Transmission System requiring the implementation of Safety Precautions on High Voltage Apparatus in accordance with the terms of T12, the Requesting Safety Coordinator requesting the implementation of Safety Precautions must communicate with the Implementing Safety Coordinator to coordinate the implementation of the Safety Precautions.

### T12.4.3 System Operation Logs

**T12.4.3.1** The Transmission System Operator of Cyprus and Users shall maintain System Operation Logs, which shall be chronological records

<!-- PAGE 219 -->

**Version 1.1.0** | **T12 – Safety Rules** | **Page 219**

---

...which shall include all messages related to safety coordination and the implementation of Precautionary Safety Measures, in accordance with T12, and have been sent or received by the Safety Coordinators. The System Switching Logs shall be retained for at least one year.

**T12.4.3.2** The procedures for the use of System Switching Logs are specified in T12 and the relevant Operating Instruction of the TSOC.

**T12.4.3.3** The forms/types of System Switching Logs to be used are included in the relevant Operating Instruction of the TSOC, and Users must procure the relevant forms from the TSOC.

**T12.4.4 Inspection of Electrical Installations of Producers, Electricity Storage Facilities, and Consumers**

Prior to granting an Operational Date for Producer Facilities and Electricity Storage Facilities in the Transmission System, an inspection of the electrical installations shall be conducted by a person or organization holding a valid Certificate of Inspection Authorization issued by the TSOC.

Candidate persons or organizations seeking to obtain a Certificate of Inspection Authorization for Producer Facilities and Electricity Storage Facilities must meet the following criteria:

a) Candidate natural persons:
   (i) Be registered in the Electrical Engineering Branch of the Scientific and Technical Chamber of Cyprus (ETEK) and hold a Certificate of Competence and a valid Certificate of Registration as a First-Class Electrical Engineer, issued by the Director of the Department of Electrical and Mechanical Services (Licensing Authority), pursuant to the provisions of the Electricity Law (Cap. 170) and Regulations as amended or replaced; and
   (ii) Have at least two years of experience in the field of execution and inspection/testing of electrical installations of Low, Medium, and High Voltage power generation systems.

b) Candidate organizations:
   (i) Hold a "Business Operating License" issued by the Director of the Department of Electrical and Mechanical Services (Licensing Authority) for undertakings engaged in electrical works, pursuant to the provisions of the Electricity Law (Cap. 170) and Regulations as amended or replaced.
   (ii) The person designated as responsible for the electrical works of the organization, under whose name the aforementioned License is issued, must be under regular employment of the organization and meet the criteria for candidate natural persons specified above.

Following the inspection of the electrical installations of the Producer or the Electricity Storage Facility, the Certificate...

<!-- PAGE 220 -->

... Inspection of Electrical Installations shall be submitted to the TSOC for approval and custody.

For Producers or Electricity Storage Facilities connected to the Distribution System, the inspection of electrical installations shall be carried out by the EAC.

In accordance with the Electricity Law (Cap. 170) and Regulations as amended or replaced, the inspection of electrical installations of Consumers connected to the Transmission System or the Distribution System shall be carried out by the EAC, whereas, for Consumers supplied without connection to the Transmission System or the Distribution System, the inspection shall be carried out by the Director of the Department of Electrical and Mechanical Services.

---

### T12.5 PRECAUTIONARY SAFETY MEASURES FOR HIGH VOLTAGE APPARATUS

#### T12.5.1 Precautionary Safety Measures

For the purpose of safety coordination concerning High Voltage Apparatus, the term **"Precautionary Safety Measures"** means Isolation and/or Earthing.

#### T12.5.2 Agreement on Precautionary Safety Measures

* **T12.5.2.1** The Requesting Safety Coordinator requesting Precautionary Safety Measures on another User System or on the Transmission System shall contact the Implementing Safety Coordinator to agree upon the Location where the Precautionary Safety Measures will be taken in accordance with the provisions of the relevant Operating Instruction of the TSOC. This agreement shall be recorded in the respective System Operation Logs.

* **T12.5.2.2** The Implementing Safety Coordinator is responsible for implementing and maintaining the necessary Precautionary Safety Measures on their own and/or another User System or connected to their side of the Transmission System, so as to achieve System Safety on the High Voltage Apparatus, as agreed between the Requesting Safety Coordinator and the Implementing Safety Coordinator. In T12.5.2.2, any reference to another User System or Transmission System shall not include the User System of the Requesting Safety Coordinator or the Transmission System referred to in T12.5.2.3.

#### T12.5.3 Implementation of Isolation

* **T12.5.3.1** When the Precautionary Safety Measures are agreed in accordance with T12.5.2, the Implementing Safety Coordinator shall establish the agreed Isolation.

* **T12.5.3.2** The Implementing Safety Coordinator shall confirm to the Requesting Safety Coordinator that the agreed Isolation has been established and shall identify the High Voltage Apparatus of the Implementing Safety Coordinator up to the Connection Point for which Isolation has been secured. The confirmation shall be recorded in the System Operation Logs and shall include:

---

| **Version 1.1.0** | **T12 – Safety Rules** | **Page 220** |
|---|---|---|

<!-- PAGE 221 -->

(a) For each Location, the identity (i.e. High Voltage Apparatus Name, nomenclature and numbering or location, as applicable) of each Isolation point.

(b) Whether Isolation has been achieved by an Isolating Device in the isolation position or by adequate physical disconnection.

(c) When an Isolating Device has been used, whether one of the following applies to the isolation position:

i) Maintained locked by Locking of the Isolating Device in the isolation position and a Warning Notice is attached. When the Isolating Device is Locked with a Safety Key, the Safety Key must be secured in a Key Safe and the Key to the Key Safe must be kept in a safe place.

ii) Maintained and/or secured by another method that complies with the Local Safety Rules of the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) or the relevant User, as applicable.

(d) There is adequate physical separation, which must comply with and be maintained in accordance with a method specified in the Local Safety Rules of the Transmission System Owner or the relevant User, as applicable, and if included in the procedures of this method, a Warning Notice must be placed at the point of separation.

**T12.5.3.3** Following confirmation of the implementation of Isolation by the Implementing Safety Coordinator and the implementation of the necessary Isolation on the User System of the Requesting Safety Coordinator or on the Transmission System, the Requesting Safety Coordinator may request the application of Earthing by the Implementing Safety Coordinator, if this has been agreed pursuant to T12.5.2.

**T12.5.4 Application of Earthing**

**T12.5.4.1** The Implementing Safety Coordinator will then establish the agreed Earthing.

**T12.5.4.2** The Implementing Safety Coordinator confirms to the Requesting Safety Coordinator that the agreed Earthing has been established, and identifies the High Voltage Apparatus of the Implementing Safety Coordinator up to the Connection Point, for which Earthing has been secured. The confirmation is recorded in the System Switching Logs and includes:

(a) For each Location, the identity (i.e. High Voltage Apparatus Name, nomenclature and numbering or location, as applicable) of each Earthing point.

(b) In relation to the Earthing Device used, whether it:

i) Is fixed and Locked in the earthing position. When the Earthing Device is Locked with a Safety Key, the Safety Key must be secured in a Key Safe and the Key to the Key Safe must be kept in a safe place.

ii) Is maintained and/or secured in position by another method that complies with the Local Safety Rules of the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) or the relevant User, as applicable.

***

| Version 1.1.0 | T12 – Safety Rules |
| :--- | ---: |
| | Page 221 |

<!-- PAGE 222 -->

**Version 1.1.0 | T12 – Safety Rules | Page 222**

---

**T12.5.4.3** The Implementing Safety Coordinator must confirm that the applied Safety Precautions remain in place until the Requesting Safety Coordinator requests their removal.

---

### T12.6 TESTS AFFECTING THE USER SYSTEM OF ANOTHER SAFETY COORDINATOR OR THE TRANSMISSION SYSTEM

**T12.6.1** The performance of a test may affect the Safety Precautions that have been applied to High Voltage Apparatus in the Transmission System or in another User System. For example, a test may include an independent Voltage test.

When the Requesting Safety Coordinator desires to carry out a test relating to the provisions of T12.6, the following steps must be followed and the confirmation of test completion must be recorded in the relevant System Operation Logs:

*(a)* Confirmation must be provided by the Implementing Safety Coordinator that:
> **i)** No person is working or testing or authorized to work or test on any part of the User System or the Transmission System or on another User System or Transmission System (other than the User System or Transmission System of the Requesting Safety Coordinator) between the Isolation Points specified in the System Operation Log concerning the proposed test, and  
> **ii)** No person shall have, as described above, authorization until the proposed test is completed (or cancelled) and the Requesting Safety Coordinator has informed the Implementing Safety Coordinator of its completion.

*(b)* The Requesting Safety Coordinator has agreed with the Implementing Safety Coordinator to permit the test on the section of the User System or Transmission System between the Isolation Points specified in the System Operation Log concerning this test, and the Isolation Points of the Requesting Safety Coordinator's User System or Transmission System.

---

**T12.6.2**

*(a)* The Requesting Safety Coordinator informs the Implementing Safety Coordinator as soon as the test is completed or cancelled, and this notification is recorded in the relevant System Operation Logs.

*(b)* When the test requires the removal of Earthing which will not be re-applied, or when Earthing is reconnected following the completion or cancellation of the test, the System Operation Logs are updated accordingly.

---

### T12.7 EMERGENCY CONDITIONS

**T12.7.1** There are circumstances where Safety Precautions must be applied in relation to unscheduled electrical connections or situations where an unforeseen risk of electrical connection between the Transmission System and the User System may exist.

<!-- PAGE 223 -->

For example, following an incident where one line comes into contact with or approaches another line outside of safety limits.

**T12.7.2** In such cases, if the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) and the involved User agree, the relevant terms of T12.5 shall apply as if the electrical connections or future connections were, solely for the purposes of T12, a single Connection Point.

**T12.7.3**
(a) The relevant Safety Coordinator shall be designated as the one associated with the nearest Connection Point to the User System or another local Connection Point, depending on the agreement between the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) and the User, following negotiations between the involved local Safety Coordinators.
(b) Local Safety Regulations are defined as the instructions pertaining to the corresponding Connection Point.

### T12.8 CANCELLATION OF PRECAUTIONARY SAFETY MEASURES

**T12.8.1** If at any time Precautionary Safety Measures may no longer apply, for any reason, the associated Safety Coordinator must immediately inform the other Safety Coordinator(s) of this situation and, if requested, provide the relevant reasons.

\<End of T12\>

# TRANSMISSION RULES

## T13 - METERING SPECIFICATIONS AND PROCEDURES

**Version 1.1.0**

---

*Version 1.1.0* | *T13 – Metering Specifications and Procedures* | *Page 224*

<!-- PAGE 225 -->

# T13 - MEASUREMENT SPECIFICATIONS AND PROCEDURES

## T13.1 INTRODUCTION

**T13.1.1** The provisions of the Measurement Specifications and Procedures define the minimum specifications required for the measurement and recording of the quantity of electrical energy transported through the Transmission System and Distribution System for commercial trading, as defined in the Law.

**T13.1.2** For settlement purposes, the quantities of electrical energy imported or exported at each Defined Metering Point must be measured and recorded by a Metering Installation that is installed, operated, and maintained as described in T13.

**T13.1.3** The Measurement Specifications and Procedures specify the minimum technical requirements for Metering Installations necessary for performing measurements and recording transported electrical energy at Defined Metering Points.

## T13.2 PURPOSE

**T13.2.1** The Measurement Specifications and Procedures set forth the procedures to be applied and the conditions to be fulfilled for the measurement and recording of measured quantities, in accordance with the Electricity Market Rules.

**T13.2.2** Specifically, regarding the Metering Installation, the Measurement Specifications and Procedures set out the conditions governing the following:
*(a)* technical, design, and operational criteria
*(b)* accuracy and calibration
*(c)* approval, certification, and testing
*(d)* Meter reading and data management

**T13.2.3** It is clarified that the retrieval of metering data at the Transmission/Distribution boundary, which falls under the responsibility of TSOC, is performed from the Medium Voltage side. These data do not constitute commercial measurements and are used for thermal loss calculation purposes as well as for adjusting the energy absorption calculation, per Load Representative, at the Transmission-Distribution boundary equipped with Profiling Meters.

## T13.3 SCOPE OF APPLICATION

**T13.3.1** The Measurement Specifications and Procedures apply to the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus and Users. In the Measurement Specifications and Procedures, Users mean the following:
*(a)* Producers
*(b)* Storage Facility Operators
*(c)* Consumers connected to the Transmission System
*(d)* Distribution System Operator
*(e)* Transmission System Owner
*(f)* Distribution System Owner

***

| Version 1.1.0 | T13 – Measurement Specifications and Procedures | Page 225 |
|---|---|---|

<!-- PAGE 226 -->

| Version 1.1.0 | T13 – Metering Specifications and Procedures | Page 226 |
|---|---|---|

***

**T13.3.2** The provisions of the Metering Specifications and Procedures apply to those Users who are obligated, in accordance with the Electricity Market Rules, to possess Meters capable of recording Metering Data for settlement purposes.

Additionally, the provisions of the Metering Specifications and Procedures apply to Producers who are obligated, pursuant to this T13.3.2, to procure and install Meters at their Power Generating Stations for the purposes of comprehensive data recording and loss calculation. Potential installation points for such additional metering installations include generator outputs for measuring gross generated energy and energy consumption metering points for auxiliary equipment, in accordance with the terms specified in the Connection Agreement. All provisions of the Metering Specifications and Procedures shall apply to these Meters, unless a derogation is granted pursuant to the procedure provided in Chapter T0. Relaxation of accuracy classes from T13.19 may be granted, as determined by TSOC or the Distribution System Operator (DSO), as applicable.

**T13.3.3** Meters installed within a Power Generating Station that are owned by the Producer must be of an approved type, specified in accordance with the provisions of T13.5. Before proceeding with the procurement and installation of its Meters, the Producer must inform TSOC or the Distribution System Operator (DSO), as applicable, regarding the specifications of the Metering Installations it intends to install, including information on the manufacturer, model, software, telecommunication connection, and wiring scheme of the Metering Installation. The Producer must obtain prior approval from TSOC or the Distribution System Operator (DSO), as applicable, to ensure that this Metering Installation is compatible with the meter management and remote reading system of TSOC or the Distribution System Operator (DSO), as applicable. These Meters shall be registered in the corresponding Metering Database in accordance with T13.7.

**T13.3.4** Provisions corresponding to those set out in T13.3.2 and T13.3.3 may also apply to Electricity Storage Installations, at the discretion of TSOC.

### T13.4 GENERAL REQUIREMENTS FOR METERING INSTALLATIONS

#### T13.4.1 SPECIFICATIONS

**T13.4.1.1** The Metering Installation and its auxiliary equipment must comply with the specifications defined in the Metering Specifications and Procedures. TSOC may deem necessary the revision of specifications for Metering Installations to the extent required for market trading and propose new specifications to CERA for approval, in cooperation with the Distribution System Operator (DSO), as applicable.

**T13.4.1.2** It is recognized that existing Metering Installations may not comply with the accuracy specifications of T13.19.6 of these Specifications and...

<!-- PAGE 227 -->

**Edition 1.1.0 T13 – Metering Specifications and Procedures**  
**Page 227**

...Metering Procedures. If harmonization is not possible, then the equipment may be granted a derogation as described in the General Provisions.

**T13.4.1.3** Metering Installations must implement communication protocols specified by the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) or by the Distribution System Operator (DSO), as applicable.

### T13.4.2 ACCURACY OF METERING INSTALLATIONS

**T13.4.2.1** Metering Installations must satisfy the accuracy limits specified for the specific type of Metering Installation in the Metering Specifications and Procedures.

**T13.4.2.2** The specifications and requirements defined in the provisions of the Metering Specifications and Procedures apply to both Main Meters and Check Meters.

### T13.5 APPROVAL, CERTIFICATION AND TESTING OF METERS

#### T13.5.1 RESPONSIBILITIES OF THE TRANSMISSION SYSTEM OPERATOR AND DISTRIBUTION SYSTEM OPERATOR

**T13.5.1.1** The Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) or the Distribution System Operator (DSO), as applicable, shall be responsible for approving the type of each Meter, checking its certification through an accredited laboratory, and verifying the execution of appropriate Tests by the Transmission System Owner or the Distribution System Owner, as applicable, to certify its compliance with the requirements of the Metering Specifications and Procedures in accordance with the scope of its authority.

**T13.5.1.2** The Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) or the Distribution System Operator (DSO), as applicable, shall maintain records for all Metering Installations installed in the Transmission System and the Distribution System.

**T13.5.1.3** These records shall also contain all technical details that the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) or the Distribution System Operator (DSO), as applicable, reasonably deems necessary. All records must be complete, accurate, and retained for as long as deemed necessary, taking into account the requirements of T13.7.5.

**T13.5.1.4** The Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) or the Distribution System Operator (DSO), as applicable, shall ensure the inspection of the sealing of Metering Installations by the Transmission System Owner or the Distribution System Owner, of the data collection equipment, the connected modems, as well as the telephone connections.

#### T13.5.2 APPROVAL AND CERTIFICATION OF METERS

**T13.5.2.1** Meters used in accordance with the Metering Specifications and Procedures must be meters of an approved type. The Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) or the Distribution System Operator...

<!-- PAGE 228 -->

**Version 1.1.0 | T13 – Specifications and Measurement Procedures | Page 228**

...of Distribution as applicable maintains a list of approved Meter types.

**T13.5.2.2** Every new Metering Arrangement is required to be submitted to the foreseen certification Tests. Tests are conducted in accordance with the IEC standards referenced in T13.14 and in accordance with European Union directives on measuring instruments, where required.

**T13.5.2.3** Issued Meter Certificates are valid for a specified period of time. The validity period of Meter Certificates may vary depending on the type of Meter and may be amended from time to time.

**T13.5.2.4** Following certification, the meter is sealed. This seal must remain intact so that the Meter remains in a certified state.

**T13.5.2.5** Meters that are decommissioned must be re-certified before being reconnected for use, in accordance with the provisions of the Specifications and Measurement Procedures.

### T13.5.3 METER TESTS AND INSPECTION

**T13.5.3.1** All Meters, current transformers, and voltage transformers are subjected to accuracy tests by the Transmission System Owner or the Distribution System Owner, as applicable, upon their initial procurement. Additionally, Tests must subsequently be conducted at specified time intervals by the Transmission System Owner or the Distribution System Owner, as applicable, to verify that their operation remains within the predefined error limits as provided in T13.19.6. Correspondingly, TSOC or the Distribution System Operator, as applicable, perform audits of the measurements. The time schedule for Tests and audits is presented in Table 13.1 below. TSOC or the Distribution System Operator, as applicable, may extend or reduce the Test period depending on the performance of the Meter. From time to time, special audits may be required.

#### Table 13.1: Time Schedule for Meter Tests

| Agreed Capacity ($P_m$) | $P_m \ge 50\text{ MVA}$ | $50 > P_m \ge 10\text{ MVA}$ | $10 > P_m \ge 1\text{ MVA}$ | $1 > P_m\text{ MVA}$ |
| :--- | :---: | :---: | :---: | :---: |
| **Audit Periodicity** | 1 year | 3 years | 5 years | 5 years |

**T13.5.3.2** Suitable Test (terminal) boxes must be provided for the Main Meters and the Verification Meters to facilitate on-site Testing. These boxes must be installed within a short distance from the Meters.

**T13.5.3.3** Meters shall also be audited outside the specified intervals (T13.5.3.1), when a deviation occurs between the Main Meter and the

<!-- PAGE 229 -->

...Verification Meter greater than 1.5 times the predefined error limit referred to in T13.19.

**T13.5.3.4** Any party bound by the Electricity Market Rules may request the execution of Tests on their Meter and shall bear the reasonable cost of the tests if it is established that the Meter is operating within the predefined error limits. The Tests shall be organized in such a way that all interested parties are informed regarding the execution of the Tests, invited to attend if they so request, and that the results of the Tests are made available to the involved Users.

**T13.5.3.5** During the execution of Tests, particular care shall be taken to avoid or minimize the duration of any interruption to metering.

**T13.5.3.6** Certified test equipment and reference standards (recognized national or international reference standards) shall be used for the execution of tests. The execution of Tests may, upon agreement with the TSOC or the Distribution System Operator as applicable, if deemed necessary, be assigned to an accredited independent laboratory.

**T13.5.3.7** When a Test indicates the existence of an error exceeding the error limits specified in T13.19, such error must be recorded prior to adjusting, repairing, or renewing the Metering Installation (or part thereof) or replacing any defective component. The Metering Installation must be returned to service and tested for operation within the predefined accuracy limits as soon as possible. In such cases, market settlement shall be conducted using alternative data in accordance with the Electricity Market Rules.

**T13.5.3.8** Records of the relevant data required to deem a test or calibration successful, in accordance with the provisions of T13.5.3, shall be maintained and updated in the Meter Database of the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) or the Distribution System Operator, as applicable.

---

### T13.6 MARKET SETTLEMENT

**T13.6.1** The Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) or the Distribution System Operator, as applicable, must guarantee that the data used for market settlement have been collected in accordance with the specifications and procedures of the Electricity Market Rules, the Transmission Rules, and the Distribution Rules, thereby bearing the characteristics of Certified Metering Data as defined in the Electricity Market Rules. Furthermore, the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) or the Distribution System Operator, as applicable, guarantees that the rules and procedures for validation, estimation, substitution, and aggregation of data required for market settlement are performed in accordance with the Electricity Market Rules, the Transmission Rules, and the Distribution Rules.

---

### T13.7 PROCUREMENT AND REGISTRATION OF METERS

**T13.7.1** The Transmission System Owner and the Distribution System Owner must procure, install, and maintain the Meters governed by the provisions of the Measurement Specifications and Procedures.

---
*Issue 1.1.0 | T13 – Measurement Specifications and Procedures | Page 229*

<!-- PAGE 230 -->

**Version 1.1.0** | **T13 – Metering Specifications and Procedures** | **Page 230**

---

and must, in cooperation with the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) or the Distribution System Operator as applicable, register them in the corresponding Metering Database.  
This provision does not apply to Meters installed pursuant to T13.3.3 within Generating Stations by the Producers themselves.

**T13.7.2** The Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) or the Distribution System Operator, as applicable, is responsible for ensuring to Users that the installation of Metering Arrangements is carried out in accordance with the provisions of the Metering Specifications and Procedures so that these Metering Arrangements can be used for settlement.  
The Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) or the Distribution System Operator, as applicable, is responsible for registering meters in the corresponding Metering Database.

**T13.7.3** The Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) or the Distribution System Operator, as applicable, maintains and updates the Metering Database in accordance with the provisions of the Electricity Market Rules. For each Metering Point, at least the following information must be recorded (Metering Database):
* (a) Meter specifications
* (b) serial numbers (as requested by the Electricity Market Rules)
* (c) access telephone numbers
* (d) pulse values
* (e) instrument transformer data
* (f) Meter certificate
* (g) connection point and site address (as requested by the Electricity Market Rules)
* (h) Consumer identification number
* (i) Producer or Energy Storage Facility identification number (as requested by the Electricity Market Rules)
* (j) installation date
* (k) installation Test details
* (l) periodic Test details
* (m) Meter access codes for the telemetry system.

**T13.7.4** The Metering Database must be updated by the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) or the Distribution System Operator, as applicable, regarding changes to Users, Metering Arrangements, and for the Deactivation or disconnection of any Metering Point. Furthermore, all information relating to each Metering Point that the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) or the Distribution System Operator, as applicable, may reasonably request shall be registered in the Metering Database.

**T13.7.5** The information specified in T13.7.3 shall be retained in the Metering Database for at least seven (7) years following the replacement or disconnection of a Meter.

### T13.8 METER DATA MANAGEMENT

#### T13.8.1 METER DATA COLLECTION

**T13.8.1.1** Data relating to the quantity of electrical energy imported or exported by each User is collected, verified, and aggregated, as

<!-- PAGE 231 -->

*Edition 1.1.0 | T13 – Metering Specifications and Procedures | Page 231*

***

...is provided for the normal operation of settlement in accordance with the Electricity Market Rules. Estimated or substitute data shall be used in accordance with the requirements specified in the Electricity Market Rules.

**T13.8.1.2** The Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) or the Distribution System Operator, as applicable, collects the necessary meter data in accordance with T13.17 for relevant Users and Supply Points from the Transmission System and the Distribution System.

### T13.8.2 METER DATA COLLECTION PROCEDURES

**T13.8.2.1** Meter data collection systems shall collect Metering Data via remote connections to Metering Installations at appropriate time intervals as defined in the Electricity Market Rules.

**T13.8.2.2** While the remote connection procedure shall normally take place automatically, it shall also be capable of being initiated manually.

**T13.8.2.3** Telemetering may be carried out via telephone call, dedicated leased telephone line, power line carrier (PLC) network, data transmission networks, or other suitable systems, using Metering and telecommunications equipment protocols specified by the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) or the Distribution System Operator, as applicable, as well as other data transmission systems required for trading and settlement purposes.

**T13.8.2.4** In the event of telecommunication media failure, Meter data shall be recorded by a locally connected device and transmitted to the central meter data collection system within a period of fifteen (15) days.

**T13.8.2.5** New Meters as well as Meters installed to replace old ones must be capable of being remotely queried for:
a) Metering Data
b) Maximum Demand and Energy Demand.

**T13.8.2.6** With the consent of the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) or the Distribution System Operator, as applicable, Inflow Energy Metering or Outflow Energy Metering may be omitted depending on the energy flow at the Metering Point.

### T13.8.3 METER MEASUREMENTS AND DATA MANAGEMENT

**T13.8.3.1** Metering Installations shall be installed in such a manner that the measurement values recorded therein are protected from any local or remote electronic access, through the use of an appropriate password and security controls.

**T13.8.3.2** Metering Data shall be collected and logged in accordance with the specifications and procedures contained in the Electricity Market Rules. T13.8.4 and T13.8.5 specify the required type of data protection. Data security, verification of...

<!-- PAGE 232 -->

...registered logs and the coordination of procedures followed in the event of Metering Equipment failure are specified in accordance with the Electricity Market Rules, the Transmission Rules, and the Distribution Rules.

**T13.8.3.3** Measurement values shall be verified and aggregated in accordance with regulations defined in the Electricity Market Rules. Values must be recorded, and alternative or estimated values shall be provided where required in accordance with the Electricity Market Rules, the Transmission Rules, and the Distribution Rules.

**T13.8.3.4** Computer architecture and communication protocols shall be compatible with the requirements of the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus or the Distribution System Operator, as applicable.

**T13.8.3.5** For each Meter, data shall be retained by the TSOC metering equipment software for at least five years.

### T13.8.4 VERIFICATION OF INSTRUMENT READINGS

**T13.8.4.1** The Transmission System Operator of Cyprus or the Distribution System Operator, as applicable, shall take the required actions, if necessary, to retrieve the Metering Data specified in T13 and in Chapter 12 of the Electricity Market Rules within the required timeframe, in cases where automatic remote metering fails.

**T13.8.4.2** The Transmission System Operator of Cyprus or the Distribution System Operator, as applicable, shall take the required actions, if necessary, to implement and manage all alternative means in order to obtain the readings of all Meters.

**T13.8.4.3** All Metering Data from Meters shall be validated and checked automatically, immediately after collection by the central electronic metering system. The validation and check procedure for Metering Data shall be carried out in accordance with the provisions of Chapter 12, paragraph 3.3 of the Electricity Market Rules, as well as in accordance with the Meters and Metering Manual. It includes the verification of measurement errors that may be due to software or equipment faults, including telecommunication system errors, as well as measurement deviations from predefined accuracy and error limits.

**T13.8.4.4** If the measurements collected from a Meter are not successfully validated following the validation and check procedure, or are unavailable, the procedure specified in Chapter 12, paragraph 3.4 of the Electricity Market Rules shall be followed, as well as in accordance with the Meters and Metering Manual.

**T13.8.4.5** The Transmission System Operator of Cyprus or the Distribution System Operator, as applicable, shall perform a manual meter reading for each Meter under its responsibility at least every twelve months, and the taking of the reading shall coincide with the end of a Settlement Period. The date and time of the reading shall be recorded.

---

| Version 1.1.0 | T13 – Specifications and Metering Procedures |
|---|---|
| Page 232 | |

<!-- PAGE 233 -->

**Version 1.1.0** | **T13 – Metering Specifications and Procedures** | **Page 233**

---

**T13.8.4.6** Within a period of twenty (20) Working Days following the annual manual meter reading as described in T13.8.4.5, a Meter Verification Statement shall be prepared. The difference between the latest and the previous manual meter reading is calculated and compared with the total energy quantity electronically registered for the same time period as provided by the telemetry system to the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) or to the Distribution System Operator (DSO), as applicable.

**T13.8.4.6** Calculations must be recorded, and any deviations exceeding 0.1% must be flagged and reported for verification. If the discrepancy is confirmed by the checks, the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) and any Users deemed necessary must be informed, and appropriate actions shall be taken in accordance with the procedures stipulated in the Electricity Market Rules.

### T13.8.5 FAILURE OF METERING EQUIPMENT

**T13.8.5.1** When Metering Data from both a Main Meter and a Check Meter are unavailable or the readings are erroneous, the Certified Metering Data shall be estimated in accordance with the provisions of Chapter 12 of the Electricity Market Rules.

**T13.8.5.2** In cases where readings are unavailable due to a fault in the Main Meter, voltage transformers, current transformers, cables, or protection devices, or in cases where the Main Meter has been proven to operate outside the specified accuracy limits, the values obtained from the Check Meter are acceptable, provided that the Check Meter is installed, the relevant Metering Data is available, and these readings have been successfully certified.

**T13.8.5.3** Priority for Metering Data is given first to the readings of the Main Meter, followed by the readings of the Check Meter, and then to the Estimated Reading. At any time, once the fault is restored and the higher-priority Metering Data is retrieved as described in this paragraph, it shall be automatically communicated to Market Participants for information, Settlement, and Reconciliation purposes as specified in the Electricity Market Rules. If the Main Meter fails, the Check Meter shall serve as the primary data source until the Main Meter is repaired or replaced.

### T13.9 REVENUE PROTECTION

**T13.9.1** The Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) or the Distribution System Operator (DSO), as applicable, shall conduct such Tests as it deems necessary to confirm that the recorded consumption at each Metering Point reflects the actual consumption by the User.

**T13.9.2** The Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) or the Distribution System Operator (DSO), in cooperation with the Transmission System Owner or the Distribution System Owner, as applicable is obliged to investigate any suspicion of illegal consumption of electricity in accordance with the relevant legislation of the country.

<!-- PAGE 234 -->

**T13.9.3** If at any point in time, a Metering Installation or part thereof is destroyed or suffers damage or, for other reasons, ceases to operate or is found to operate outside the predetermined accuracy limits, it must be adjusted in a timely manner, replaced, or repaired, or any damaged component must be replaced so as to ensure that the Metering Installation is operational and operates within the predetermined accuracy limits, under all conditions, in the shortest time possible. Users, owners, operators, and any person having access in any way to the Metering Installation are obligated to handle the Metering Installation in a safe and careful manner and to take all necessary measures to avoid causing damage to it. Users shall immediately notify the supplier of the Metering Installation in writing of any damage to the Metering Installation, regardless of the cause that produced it.

### T13.10 ACCESS

**T13.10.1** Each User shall grant to the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) or the Distribution System Operator (DSO), as applicable, to their employees, representatives, and subcontractors, rights of access, transit, and stay in any part of its facilities, provided that this is required for the fulfillment of the obligations provided for in the Metering Specifications and Procedures.

**T13.10.2** The right of access in this case includes the right to transport onto the User's facilities vehicles, machinery, maintenance equipment, or other materials reasonably required for the fulfillment of the obligations provided for in the Metering Specifications and Procedures.

**T13.10.3** Each User must guarantee the execution and periodic renewal of all necessary agreements and terms that are essential or facilitate the safe exercise of the right of access.

### T13.11 DISPUTES

Disputes arising from the implementation of T13 shall be resolved in accordance with the provisions of the Electricity Market Rules.

### T13.12 INFORMATION

The Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) or the Distribution System Operator (DSO), as applicable, shall have guaranteed access to all information concerning the Metering Installations which the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) or the Distribution System Operator (DSO), as applicable, deems necessary to ensure the proper operation of the Settlement System and the implementation of these Metering Specifications and Procedures.

### T13.13 NOTICES

***

| Version 1.1.0 | T13 – Metering Specifications and Procedures | Page 234 |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |

<!-- PAGE 235 -->

**T13 – Measurement Specifications and Procedures**  
*Version 1.1.0 | Page 235*

***

Any information regarding a new User, a change in a User, or a change in a Metering Arrangement must be notified to the Transmission System Operator Cyprus (TSOC) or the Distribution System Operator, as applicable, as specified.

### T13.14 APPLICABLE STANDARDS

The standards concerning meters and measurements in the Electricity System are specified in detail in the relevant Meters and Measurements Manual.

### T13.15 METERING POINT

**T13.15.1** The location of the Defined Metering Point is specified in the User's Connection Agreement.

**T13.15.2** Following approval by the Transmission System Operator Cyprus or the Distribution System Operator, as applicable, the Actual Metering Point may differ from the Defined Metering Point. In such cases, the accuracy requirements specified in the Measurement Specifications and Procedures apply at the Defined Metering Point, and clause T13.19.5 applies.

**T13.15.3** Metering Arrangements must be capable of calculating the flow of Active and Reactive Energy during each Settlement Period for each Defined Metering Point.

**T13.15.4** The characteristics of current transformers, voltage transformers, and power transformers, the meter registration details, and any other parameter related to the Metering Arrangements must be recorded in the corresponding Meter Database.

### T13.16 MAIN METERS AND CHECK METERS

**T13.16.1** User connections at High or Medium Voltage, as well as all User connections participating in the Electricity Market, must be equipped with Main Meters and Check Meters. These meters shall be connected to independent windings of voltage transformers and current transformers.

An exception is made for Meters installed on the Medium Voltage circuit breakers of transformers at Transmission Substations, which are Main Meters only. These Meters are necessary for the TSOC to perform checks using its own methodology, which is described in the Meters and Measurements Manual.

**T13.16.2** The windings of voltage transformers and current transformers, as well as the connection cables of such windings to the Main Meters, shall serve exclusively the connections mentioned above. These cables and connections shall be securely sealed.

**T13.16.3** The windings of voltage transformers and current transformers and the connection cables of such windings to Check Meters are permitted to be used for other purposes, provided that the overall accuracy requirements are met and the value of the additional burden will not

<!-- PAGE 236 -->

...exceed the VA capacity or otherwise, and can be controlled by the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC).

**T13.16.4** The Main Meter, the Check Meter, and any additional connected loads shall have independent fuses in their power supply from the Voltage Transformers.

### T13.17 MEASURED QUANTITIES

**T13.17.1** For each independent circuit, the User's Metering Arrangement shall be capable of measuring the following quantities:
(a) incoming energy quantity in kWh
(b) incoming quantity in kVArh.

For each independent circuit, the Metering Arrangement of each Producer, each Electricity Storage Facility, and each Consumer with Self-Generation shall additionally be capable of measuring the following quantities:
(a) outgoing energy quantity in kWh
(b) outgoing quantity in kVArh.

**T13.17.2** For each independent circuit and for each Trading Period, the User Metering Arrangement shall be capable of measuring the following Metering Data:
(a) incoming kW
(b) incoming kVAr.

For each independent circuit and for each Trading Period, the Metering Arrangement of each Producer, Electricity Storage Facility, and each Consumer with Self-Generation shall additionally be capable of measuring the following Metering Data:
(a) outgoing kW
(b) outgoing kVAr.

**T13.17.3** The Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) or the Distribution System Operator (DSO), as applicable, shall define for each Designated Metering Point the Metering Data that must be provided for carrying out settlement in accordance with the Electricity Market Rules.

### T13.18 METERING ARRANGEMENT STANDARDS

**T13.18.1** Each Metering Arrangement shall comply with the specifications provided in T13.14. These specifications may be revised at regular intervals with the agreement of CERA in order to take into account potential technological developments or industry requirements.

**T13.18.2** Each Meter shall be placed in an enclosure designed according to its manufacturer's requirements—which shall be available from the Transmission System Owner or Distribution System Owner, as applicable—for the environmental conditions. Design measures shall ensure at least protection against moisture, dust ingress, and physical damage including vibrations, and shall allow appropriate temperature control. Furthermore, the enclosure must be lockable and sealable to prevent unauthorized access.

---
*Version 1.1.0 | T13 – Metering Specifications and Procedures | Page 236*

<!-- PAGE 237 -->

**Version 1.1.0 | T13 – Metering Specifications and Procedures**
**Page 237**

***

**T13.18.3** For the execution of Measurements, one current transformer and one voltage transformer must be provided, as specified in the European standards referenced in T13.14.

**T13.18.4** Where a combined unit of voltage transformers and current transformers is installed, an "accuracy test" shall be performed in accordance with standard EN 61869-4, in order to account for their interaction phenomena.

**T13.18.5** Meters, in accordance with applicable European standards referenced in T13.14, must be connected to a voltage transformer and a current transformer and must be housed in a securely sealable Metering enclosure (T13.10), adjacent to all associated data recording and telecommunication equipment.

**T13.18.6** Data storage shall be performed either internally within the Meter or externally via a data logger.

**T13.18.7** Meters that provide data to external data loggers must feature one output for each measured quantity.

**T13.18.8** New Meters and Meters installed to replace existing ones must possess at least eight (8) Energy registers and four (4) Power Demand registers.

**T13.18.9** All Meters must feature a cumulative energy Meter Register that prevents tampering with the registered quantity for each measurement. The Meter Register shall not be reset more than once during a standard metering cycle.

**T13.18.10** All data communication equipment must comply with the relevant international standards and recommendations of the International Telecommunication Union (ITU) regarding data transmission via telecommunication systems.

**T13.18.11** Special insulation requirements may also apply in accordance with Good Professional Practice rules and in line with the specific requirements of the telecommunications service provider.

### T13.19 EQUIPMENT ACCURACY AND ERROR LIMITS

**T13.19.1** The accuracy of the various components of the Metering Installation must comply with the relevant European EN standards, or in cases where such standards do not exist for a specific subject, with international IEC standards.

**T13.19.2** For the purpose of the provisions of the Metering Specifications and Procedures, the nominal power of the circuit in MVA is determined by the primary element of the circuit with the lowest rated capacity (e.g., rating of transformers, rating of lines, etc.). The characteristics of the Metering Installation and the accuracy requirements must take into account any future upgrade of the installed primary facility. For this assessment, the maximum continuous ratings of the primary facility shall be used.

***
**Version 1.1.0 | T13 – Metering Specifications and Procedures**
**Page 237**

<!-- PAGE 238 -->

**Version 1.1.0** | **T13 – Specifications and Procedures for Metering**
**Page 238**

---

**T13.19.3** The accuracy class or other equivalent parameter shall be defined in accordance with the transfer capacity of the connection in MVA and shall at least be as follows:

##### Table 13.2: Accuracy classes of equipment

| Device Type | Connection Capacity (CC): CC > 50 MVA | Connection Capacity (CC): 50 MVA ≥ CC > 10 MVA | Connection Capacity (CC): 10 MVA ≥ CC > 1 MVA | Connection Capacity (CC): 1 MVA ≥ CC |
| :--- | :---: | :---: | :---: | :---: |
| **Current Transformer** | 0.2S | 0.2S | 0.2S | 0.2S |
| **Voltage Transformer** | 0.2 | 0.5 | 0.5 | 0.5 |
| **Meters** | 0.2S | 0.5S | 1.0 | 2.0 |

---

**T13.19.4** Certificates for voltage transformers, current transformers, and Metering Tests shall be made available for inspection by the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) or the Distribution System Operator (DSO), as applicable.

**T13.19.5** When the Actual Metering Point and the Defined Metering Point differ, then, where necessary, power transformer and/or line losses shall be compensated so that the overall required accuracy at the Defined Metering Point is fulfilled. Compensation may be applied locally within the Metering Installation or remotely. In both cases, the compensation factors and relevant justifications shall be recorded. These records shall be made available for inspection by the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC).

**T13.19.6** For the measurement of Active and Reactive Energy, each Metering Installation shall be tested and calibrated by the Transmission System Owner or the Distribution System Owner, as applicable, so as to operate within the overall error limits specified in Table 13.3 below, taking into account potential errors of voltage transformers, current transformers, and the resistance of wiring or protection circuits. The adjustment mechanism shall be based on recognized national or international standards. The auditing and control of measurements is the responsibility of the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) or the Distribution System Operator (DSO), as applicable, as is the determination of the relevant methodology.

<!-- PAGE 239 -->

**T13.19.7** The values that must be achieved in order for the Testing and calibration to be considered successful in accordance with the requirements of T13.9.6 are recorded in files that must be maintained in the corresponding Meter Database.

**T13.19.8** Where existing instrument transformers do not meet all the conditions of the Metering Specifications and Procedures, these shall become acceptable, provided that each of the following conditions is met and subject to the approval of CERA:

(a) When a significant modification is made to the electrical installation, new instrument transformers shall be installed (fully compliant with the provisions of the Metering Specifications and Procedures).

(b) Where instrument transformers are not used exclusively for Settlement Metering, the additional burdens shall be quantified and taken into account in the calibration and testing. Additional burdens are not permitted to change without informing the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) or the Distribution System Operator, as applicable.

(c) The Main Meter, the Verification Meter, and the additional burdens must be supplied by the voltage transformers via independent fuses.

**T13.20 STORAGE OF INFORMATION**

<!-- PAGE 240 -->

**Version 1.1.0** | **T13 – Metering Specifications and Procedures** | **Page 240**

---

The means used for storing Meter values shall meet the following requirements:

(a) Possess a storage capacity of 96 periods per day for at least 60 days and for all Metering Data.  
(b) Stored Metering Data shall correspond to integer values of kW or kVAr or pulse measurements and shall have a resolution better than 0.1% (at full load).  
(c) The accuracy of energy values calculated from Metering Data shall remain within limits of ±0.1% of the value of the measured energy from the corresponding Meter.  
(d) The value of each measured energy quantity in a Settlement Period that was not stored for that specific Settlement Period shall be carried forward to the next Settlement Period.  
(e) In the event of a power supply failure of the Metering Equipment, the Metering Equipment shall protect all information stored up to the time of failure and maintain time accuracy in accordance with T13.21.  
(f) To address sustained power supply failures, the clock, calendar, and all data shall be supported for a period of ten days without connection to an external power source.  
(g) "Readout" functions shall not delete or modify stored measurements.  
(h) Upon request from the Data Collection System, each Metering Equipment shall be capable of providing any portion of the data stored within it.  

### T13.21 SYNCHRONIZATION

**T13.21.1** Each Metering Equipment shall be set according to Eastern European Time (GMT + 2 hours) and adjusted annually to Daylight Saving Time.

**T13.21.2** Time adjustments may be performed when required following communication with the Data Collection System. The Transmission System Owner or the Distribution System Owner is obliged to provide access to the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) or the Distribution System Operator, as applicable, to carry out these specific adjustments, which are executed on a daily basis.

**T13.21.3** Each Settlement Period shall begin within ± 10 seconds of actual time. The duration of each Settlement Period shall be accurate within limits of ± 0.1%, unless a Synchronization has occurred during that period.

### T13.22 ACCESS AND SEALING OF METERS

**T13.22.1** Each Metering Equipment together with its corresponding telecommunications equipment shall be placed in a secure Metering cabinet, installed in an easily accessible area, free of obstacles, and well illuminated with artificial lighting.

**T13.22.2** The metering cabinet shall feature a lock, shall be unobstructed at the front, and shall be sealed accordingly by the Transmission System Owner or the Distribution System Owner. Seal inspection is conducted in accordance with procedures defined from time to time by the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) or the Distribution System Operator, as applicable.

<!-- PAGE 241 -->

**Edition 1.1.0** | **T13 – Metering Specifications and Procedures** | **Page 241**

Unsealing is permitted only by or following the prior consent of the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) or the Distribution System Operator (DSO), as applicable, or in an emergency situation, after which the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) or the Distribution System Operator (DSO), as applicable, must be notified as soon as possible.

**T13.22.3** Access to the Metering Installation and the information contained therein, as well as to the associated telecommunications equipment, shall be conducted in accordance with the procedures specified by the Transmission System Operator of Cyprus (TSOC) or the Distribution System Operator (DSO), as applicable.

**T13.22.4** All Meter readings must at all times be easily legible.

**T13.22.5** The Transmission System Owner or the Distribution System Owner, as applicable, shall check the placement of their seals and sealing tools, and maintain an accurate log of sealing tools as well as the authorized users to whom they are issued. The Transmission System Owner or the Distribution System Owner, as applicable, shall inspect the sealing tools annually.

<End of T13>
